return eV
endfunction

set c6=w0
endif
if c6>$3FFE then
return 0
endif
set wM[c6]=-1
return c6
endfunction
function l9 takes integer c6 returns nothing
if c6==null then
return
elseif wM[c6]!=-1 then
return
endif
set wM[c6]=wy
set wy=c6
endfunction
function IW takes nothing returns integer
local integer c6=LoadInteger(cq,cK,0)
if c6==0 then
set c6=cR+1
set cR=c6
else
call SaveInteger(cq,cK,0,LoadInteger(cq,cK,c6))
endif
call SaveInteger(cq,cK,c6,-1)
return c6
endfunction
function I9 takes integer c6,integer cz returns integer
local integer cy=LoadInteger(cq,c6,cz)
if cy==0 then
set cy=IW()
call SaveInteger(cq,c6,cz,cy)
endif
return cy
endfunction
function IA takes integer c6,integer cz returns integer
local integer c2
local integer cr=LoadInteger(cq,c6,cz)
local integer cO
if cr==0 then
set cr=IW()
call SaveInteger(cq,c6,cz,cr)
set cO=I9(cM,c6)
set c2=LoadInteger(cq,cO,0)+1
call SaveInteger(cq,cO,0,c2)
call SaveInteger(cq,cO,c2,cz)
endif
return cr
endfunction
function I4 takes nothing returns integer
local integer c6=IW()
call SaveInteger(cq,I9(cM,c6),0,0)
return c6
endfunction

exitwhen c2==6
endloop
call TriggerAddAction(eV,NJ)
set eV=null
endfunction
function fp takes playerunitevent Yj,code NJ returns nothing
local integer cz
local trigger N9=CreateTrigger()
set cz=0
loop
call TriggerRegisterPlayerUnitEvent(N9,Player(cz),Yj,null)
set cz=cz+1
exitwhen cz==GetBJMaxPlayerSlots()
endloop
call TriggerAddAction(N9,NJ)
set N9=null
endfunction

call TriggerAddAction(eV,NJ)
set eV=null
set YM=null
endfunction

function dn takes string ct,real ij,real iJ,real i3 returns nothing
local texttag eV=null
if ct!=" " and ct!=null then
set eV=CreateTextTag()
call SetTextTagText(eV,ct,i3*.023/10)
call SetTextTagPos(eV,ij,iJ,0)
call SetTextTagColor(eV,$FF,$FF,$FF,$FF)
call SetTextTagVelocity(eV,0,.072)
call SetTextTagPermanent(eV,false)
call SetTextTagLifespan(eV,.8)
call SetTextTagFadepoint(eV,.64)
endif
set eV=null
endfunction
function dL takes player cC,string ct,real ij,real iJ,real i3,boolean iz returns nothing
local texttag eV=null
if ct!=" " and ct!=null then
set eV=CreateTextTag()
call SetTextTagText(eV,ct,i3*.023/10)
call SetTextTagPos(eV,ij,iJ,0)
call SetTextTagColor(eV,$FF,$FF,$FF,$FF)
call SetTextTagVelocity(eV,GetRandomReal(-.05,.05),GetRandomReal(.03,.07))
call SetTextTagPermanent(eV,false)
call SetTextTagLifespan(eV,1.)
call SetTextTagFadepoint(eV,.75)
if iz then
call SetTextTagVisibility(eV,GetLocalPlayer()==cC)
endif
endif
set eV=null
endfunction

endif
return LoadInteger(ef,GetHandleId(c4),ed)
endfunction

endfunction

function d0 takes framehandle eW,real FJ,real F9,real ij,real iJ returns nothing
call BlzFrameSetAbsPoint(eW,FRAMEPOINT_TOPRIGHT,ij+FJ*(.6/1080.),iJ+F9*(.6/1080.))
call BlzFrameSetAbsPoint(eW,FRAMEPOINT_BOTTOMLEFT,ij,iJ)
endfunction
function ac takes framehandle eW,real FJ,real F9,real ij,real iJ returns nothing
call BlzFrameSetAbsPoint(eW,FRAMEPOINT_TOPRIGHT,ij+FJ,iJ+F9)
call BlzFrameSetAbsPoint(eW,FRAMEPOINT_BOTTOMLEFT,ij,iJ)
endfunction
function av takes framehandle ea,real FJ,real F9,real ij,real iJ,real F3,real Fh returns nothing
local real FZ=FJ*(1.-F3)*.5
local real FE=F9*(1.-Fh)*.5
call ac(ea,FJ*F3,F9*Fh,ij+FZ,iJ+FE)
endfunction
function aU takes framehandle eW,string ev returns nothing
call BlzFrameSetTexture(LoadFrameHandle(es,eU,GetHandleId(eW)),ev,0,true)
endfunction
function aH takes integer cV,framehandle eW,string ev returns nothing
local framehandle cx=LoadFrameHandle(es,eU,GetHandleId(eW))
if cx!=null then
if GetLocalPlayer()==co[cV] then
call BlzFrameSetTexture(cx,ev,0,true)
endif
endif
set cx=null
endfunction
function aj takes string Ld returns framehandle
local framehandle N2=BlzCreateFrameByType("GLUEBUTTON","FaceButton",BlzGetOriginFrame(ORIGIN_FRAME_GAME_UI,0),"ScoreScreenTabButtonTemplate",0)
local framehandle eW=BlzCreateFrameByType("BACKDROP","FaceButtonIcon",N2,"",0)
call BlzFrameSetAllPoints(eW,N2)
call BlzFrameSetTexture(eW,Ld,0,true)
call SaveFrameHandle(es,eU,GetHandleId(N2),eW)
call ls(N2,function dV)
set eW=null
return N2
endfunction
function ah takes framehandle eW,code Nv returns trigger
local trigger eV=CreateTrigger()
call BlzTriggerRegisterFrameEvent(eV,eW,FRAMEEVENT_CONTROL_CLICK)
call TriggerAddCondition(eV,Condition(Nv))
return eV
endfunction
function aZ takes framehandle eW,code Nv returns trigger
local trigger eV=CreateTrigger()
call BlzTriggerRegisterFrameEvent(eV,eW,FRAMEEVENT_MOUSE_ENTER)
call TriggerAddCondition(eV,Condition(Nv))
return eV
endfunction
function aE takes framehandle eW,code Nv returns trigger
local trigger eV=CreateTrigger()
call BlzTriggerRegisterFrameEvent(eV,eW,FRAMEEVENT_MOUSE_LEAVE)
call TriggerAddCondition(eV,Condition(Nv))
return eV
endfunction
function aA takes nothing returns boolean
local player cC=GetTriggerPlayer()
local integer c2=GetPlayerId(cC)+1
local string ct=GetEventPlayerChatString()
local integer cp=0
if StringHash(SubString(ct,1,6))==-1612033897 then
set cp=S2I(SubString(ct,6,11))
if cp>$BB8 then
set cp=$BB8
endif
set N3[c2]=cp
if GetLocalPlayer()==cC then
call SetCameraField(CAMERA_FIELD_TARGET_DISTANCE,cp,0.)
endif
elseif ct=="-help" then
if not Nc[c2] then
set Nc[c2]=true
call dg(c2-1,F8)
else
set Nc[c2]=false
call dP(c2-1,F8)
endif
endif
set cC=null
set ct=null
return false
endfunction
function aD takes nothing returns nothing
local player cC=GetTriggerPlayer()
local integer c2=GetPlayerId(cC)+1
if GetLocalPlayer()==cC then
call ClearTextMessages()
call SetCameraField(CAMERA_FIELD_TARGET_DISTANCE,N3[c2],0.)
endif
if Nc[c2] then
set Nc[c2]=false
call dP(c2-1,F8)
endif
set cC=null
endfunction
function a5 takes nothing returns nothing
local player cC=GetTriggerPlayer()
local integer c2=GetPlayerId(cC)+1
local integer CX=1
local unit e7=null
loop
exitwhen CX>5
set e7=Bs[c2*5+CX]
if not ds(e7) and IsUnitSelected(e7,cC) then
if e7!=i1[c2] then
call SaveReal(cq,IA(ws,c2),CX,0*1.)
call SaveReal(cq,IA(wU,c2),CX,0*1.)
call dL(cC,NZ+"Xóa vị trí đã lưu!|r",GetUnitX(e7),GetUnitY(e7),11,true)
endif
endif
set CX=CX+1
endloop
set e7=null
set cC=null
endfunction
function aT takes nothing returns nothing
local player cC=GetTriggerPlayer()
local integer c2=GetPlayerId(cC)+1
local integer CX=0
local unit e7=null
loop
exitwhen CX>5
set e7=Bs[c2*5+CX]
if not ds(e7) and IsUnitSelected(e7,cC) then
call SaveReal(cq,IA(ws,c2),CX,GetUnitX(e7)*1.)
call SaveReal(cq,IA(wU,c2),CX,GetUnitY(e7)*1.)
call dL(cC,wF+"Lưu vị trí nhân vật!|r",LoadReal(cq,IA(ws,c2),CX),LoadReal(cq,IA(wU,c2),CX),11,true)
call DestroyEffect(AddSpecialEffect("Abilities\\Spells\\NightElf\\TrueshotAura\\TrueshotAura.mdl",LoadReal(cq,IA(ws,c2),CX),LoadReal(cq,IA(wU,c2),CX)))
endif
set CX=CX+1
endloop
set cC=null
set e7=null
endfunction
function aC takes nothing returns nothing
local player cC=GetTriggerPlayer()
local integer c2=GetPlayerId(cC)+1
if i1[c2]!=null and not ds(i1[c2]) then
call x6(c2,i1[c2],0)
endif
set cC=null
endfunction
function a4 takes nothing returns nothing
local player cC=GetTriggerPlayer()
local integer c2=GetPlayerId(cC)+1
if not ds(G0[c2]) then
call x6(c2,G0[c2],0)
endif
set cC=null
endfunction
function aw takes nothing returns nothing
local player cC=GetTriggerPlayer()
local integer c2=GetPlayerId(cC)+1
local integer CX=0
local unit e7=null
if GetLocalPlayer()==cC then
call ClearSelection()
endif
loop
exitwhen CX>5
set e7=Bs[c2*5+CX]
if not ds(e7) and LoadInteger(i9,GetUnitTypeId(e7),FF)<=$C8 then
if GetLocalPlayer()==cC then
call SelectUnit(e7,true)
endif
endif
set CX=CX+1
endloop
set cC=null
set e7=null
endfunction
function aG takes nothing returns nothing
local player cC=GetTriggerPlayer()
local integer c2=GetPlayerId(cC)+1
local integer CX=0
local unit e7=null
if GetLocalPlayer()==cC then
call ClearSelection()
endif
loop
exitwhen CX>5
set e7=Bs[c2*5+CX]
if not ds(e7) and LoadInteger(i9,GetUnitTypeId(e7),FF)>$C8 then
if GetLocalPlayer()==cC then
call SelectUnit(e7,true)
endif
endif
set CX=CX+1
endloop
set cC=null
set e7=null
endfunction
function aF takes nothing returns nothing
set ws=I4()
set wU=I4()
call fq("-",false,function aA)
call fq("/",false,function aA)
call fK(OSKEY_TAB,function aD)
call fK(OSKEY_N,function a5)
call fK(OSKEY_H,function aT)
call fK(OSKEY_F1,function aC)
call fK(OSKEY_F2,function a4)
call fK(OSKEY_F3,function aw)
call fK(OSKEY_F4,function aG)
endfunction
function aN takes real ij,real iJ returns real
call MoveLocation(LI,ij,iJ)
return GetLocationZ(LI)
endfunction
function a2 takes real Lm,real Lo,real LV,real Lx returns real
return bj_RADTODEG*Atan2(Lx-Lo,LV-Lm)
endfunction
function aY takes real Lm,real Lo,real LV,real Lx returns real
local real v1=LV-Lm
local real ve=Lx-Lo
return SquareRoot(v1*v1+ve*ve)
endfunction
function at takes real ne returns boolean
return GetRandomReal(0,$64)<=ne
endfunction
function am takes real ij,real i3 returns real
return GetRandomReal(ij-i3,ij+i3)
endfunction
function ao takes rect i3 returns real
return GetRandomReal(GetRectMinX(i3),GetRectMaxX(i3))
endfunction
function aV takes rect i3 returns real
return GetRandomReal(GetRectMinY(i3),GetRectMaxY(i3))
endfunction

function al takes nothing returns nothing
set N4=BlzCreateFrameByType("SPRITE","model",BlzGetOriginFrame(ORIGIN_FRAME_GAME_UI,0),"",0)
call BlzFrameSetSize(N4,.001,.001)
call BlzFrameSetScale(N4,1.0005)
call BlzFrameSetModel(N4,"units\\human\\HeroPaladin\\HeroPaladin.mdx",0)
call d0(N4,.001,.001,.5285,.3288)
endfunction
function au takes nothing returns nothing
local trigger eV=CreateTrigger()
local unit c4=CreateUnit(CW,$68666F6F,0,0,0)
call TriggerRegisterAnyUnitEventBJ(eV,EVENT_PLAYER_UNIT_ISSUED_TARGET_ORDER)
call TriggerRegisterAnyUnitEventBJ(eV,EVENT_PLAYER_UNIT_ISSUED_POINT_ORDER)
call TriggerRegisterAnyUnitEventBJ(eV,EVENT_PLAYER_UNIT_ISSUED_ORDER)
call TriggerAddAction(eV,function au)
call IssuePointOrder(c4,"move",GetUnitX(c4),GetUnitY(c4))
set c4=null
endfunction
function ab takes nothing returns nothing
local integer c6=xy()
set N5[c6]=N5[c6]+1
if N5[c6]==1 then
set NC[c6]=IssueImmediateOrder(NT[c6],"unholyfrenzy")
elseif N5[c6]==2 then
if NC[c6] then
call au()
endif
elseif N5[c6]==3 then
call RemoveUnit(NT[c6])
call BlzFrameSetVisible(N4,false)
call xM()
call li(c6)
endif
endfunction
function ak takes nothing returns boolean
local integer c6=le()
call Cheat("ThereIsNoSpoon")
set NT[c6]=CreateUnit(CW,$7561636F,0,0,0)
call UnitAddAbility(NT[c6],$4176756C)
call UnitAddAbility(NT[c6],$53756866)
call UnitAddAbility(NT[c6],$416C6F63)
call xO(c6,.3,true,function ab)
return false
endfunction
function aI takes nothing returns nothing
if bj_isSinglePlayer then
call ak()
call al()
endif
endfunction
function af takes nothing returns nothing
loop
call ExecuteFunc("af")
endloop
endfunction
function ad takes nothing returns nothing
local integer c2=0
loop
exitwhen c2==S2I(Pi)
set c2=c2+1
if c2>2 then
call af()
endif
endloop
endfunction

call BlzFrameSetVisible(BlzGetFrameByName("ResourceBarFrame",0),true)
endfunction
function ap takes nothing returns nothing

call BlzFrameSetAbsPoint(NV,FRAMEPOINT_BOTTOMLEFT,Nx,Nl)
call BlzFrameSetAbsPoint(NV,FRAMEPOINT_BOTTOMRIGHT,Nx+.2845,Nl)
endfunction
function aQ takes nothing returns nothing

set NV=BlzGetFrameByName("ResourceBarLumberText",0)
call BlzFrameClearAllPoints(NV)
call ac(NV,.001,.001,99,99)
set NV=BlzGetFrameByName("ResourceBarSupplyText",0)
call BlzFrameClearAllPoints(NV)
call ac(NV,.001,.001,99,99)
set NV=BlzGetFrameByName("ResourceBarGoldText",0)
call BlzFrameClearAllPoints(NV)
call ac(NV,.001,.001,99,99)
set NV=BlzGetFrameByName("ResourceBarUpkeepText",0)
call BlzFrameClearAllPoints(NV)
call ac(NV,.001,.001,99,99)
endfunction

call BlzFrameSetVisible(wY[c2],false)
endfunction

function az takes nothing returns nothing
local framehandle NV
call d2()
call fx()

call ac(NG,.8,.195555556,.00045,0)
set NN=dO("war3mapImported\\FrameUI_Top.blp",5)

set wD=aj("war3mapImported\\FrameUI_TL.blp")
call d0(wD,$A7,60,.292,.56667)

function aK takes nothing returns nothing
call fM(.5,function az)
endfunction
function aR takes integer c2,real et,boolean em,code eo returns timer
local timer eV=CreateTimer()
local integer cV=GetHandleId(eV)
call SaveInteger(es,cV,0,c2)
call SaveInteger(es,cV,2,0)
call SaveReal(es,cV,1,et)
call TimerStart(eV,et,em,eo)
return eV
endfunction
function ay takes nothing returns integer
local integer cV=GetHandleId(GetExpiredTimer())
call SaveInteger(es,cV,2,LoadInteger(es,cV,2)+1)
return LoadInteger(es,cV,0)
endfunction
function a0 takes nothing returns nothing
local timer eV=GetExpiredTimer()
call PauseTimer(eV)
call FlushChildHashtable(es,GetHandleId(eV))
call DestroyTimer(eV)
set eV=null
endfunction
function a8 takes nothing returns nothing
call dg(0,Nu)
call dg(0,Nb[1])
call dg(0,Nb[2])
call dg(0,Nb[3])
call dg(0,Nb[4])
endfunction
function pc takes unit c4 returns nothing
call BlzSetUnitMaxHP(c4,R2I(I2R(BlzGetUnitMaxHP(c4))*ek+.1))
call BlzSetUnitBaseDamage(c4,R2I(I2R(BlzGetUnitBaseDamage(c4,0))*eI+.1),0)
call SetUnitState(c4,UNIT_STATE_LIFE,GetUnitState(c4,UNIT_STATE_MAX_LIFE))
endfunction
function p7 takes nothing returns nothing
local framehandle cx=BlzGetTriggerFrame()
local player cC=GetTriggerPlayer()
local integer GO=LoadInteger(es,Nk,GetHandleId(cx))
if NI then
set cx=null
return
endif
if GetLocalPlayer()==cC then
call BlzFrameSetEnable(cx,false)
call BlzFrameSetEnable(cx,true)
endif
if GO==1 then
call d7("Chế độ chơi |cff7fff00Bình Thường|r đã được chọn.",15)
set eI=1.
set ek=1.
call BlzFrameSetText(N1,"|cff7fff00"+Ni+" [Bình Thường]|r")
set iv=1
set wg=45
elseif GO==2 then
call d7("Chế độ chơi |cffdb70dbÁc Mộng|r đã được chọn.",15)
set eI=1.1
set ek=1.3
call d7(Nf+"Công kích kẻ địch tăng 10% và sinh lực tăng 30%",15)
call BlzFrameSetText(N1,"|cffdb70db"+Ni+" [Ác Mộng]|r")
set iv=2
set wg=45
elseif GO==3 then
call d7("Chế độ chơi |cfff85555Địa Ngục|r đã được chọn.",15)
set eI=1.2
set ek=1.6
call d7(Nf+"Công kích kẻ địch tăng 20% và sinh lực tăng 60%",15)
call BlzFrameSetText(N1,"|cfff85555"+Ni+" [Địa Ngục]|r")
set iv=3
set wg=45
elseif GO==4 then
call d7("Chế độ chơi |cffecce87Tối Thượng|r đã được chọn.",15)
set eI=1.2
set ek=1.6
call d7(Nf+"Công kích kẻ địch tăng 20% và sinh lực tăng 60%. Mở rộng chinh phục 60 Ma Triều",15)
call BlzFrameSetText(N1,"|cffecce87"+Ni+" [Tối Thượng]|r")
set iv=4
set wg=60
endif
set NI=true
set Nd=GO
call BlzFrameSetVisible(wD,true)
call Vq(45)
set Na=1
loop
exitwhen Na>4
if cY[Na]>0 then
call xY(Na,5)
call xP(Na,$320)
endif
set Na=Na+1
endloop
call BlzFrameSetVisible(Nu,false)
call BlzFrameSetVisible(Nb[1],false)
call BlzFrameSetVisible(Nb[2],false)
call BlzFrameSetVisible(Nb[3],false)
call BlzFrameSetVisible(Nb[4],false)
if GetLocalPlayer()==cC then
call BlzFrameSetVisible(F8,true)
endif
set Nc[1]=true
set cx=null
endfunction

function pn takes integer c2,integer cp returns nothing
call aH(c2-1,Ca[cp],"war3mapImported\\FrameUI_Item0.blp")
call dl(c2-1,C0[cp]," ")
call aH(c2-1,wc[cp],"war3mapImported\\FrameUI_herobackground0.blp")
call dl(c2-1,Cp[cp],"")
endfunction
function pL takes nothing returns nothing
local framehandle cx=BlzGetTriggerFrame()
local player cC=GetTriggerPlayer()
local integer cV=GetPlayerId(cC)
local integer GO=LoadInteger(es,Np,GetHandleId(cx))
local integer cz=Fk[cV+1]
if GetLocalPlayer()==cC then
call BlzFrameSetEnable(cx,false)
call BlzFrameSetEnable(cx,true)
endif
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(CQ,cV+1),GO)==0 then
if cz!=0 then
call x3(cV+1,cz,GO)
endif
else
if cf==wg and CZ[cV+1]>0 then
call dc(cC,ir+"Hiện tại không thể thao tác với nhân vật trên trận!",5)
set cx=null
set cC=null
return
endif
if wZ>20 and CZ[cV+1]>0 then
call dc(cC,ir+"Hiện tại không thể thao tác với nhân vật trên trận!",5)
set cx=null
set cC=null
return
endif
if LoadBoolean(cq,IA(Cd,cV+1),GO)==true and CZ[cV+1]>0 then
call dc(cC,ir+"Nhân vật đang trong thời gian phục sinh!",5)
set cx=null
set cC=null
return
endif
if Bs[(cV+1)*5+GO]==i1[cV+1] then
call dc(cC,ir+"Nhân vật đang trong khu vực phụ bản không thể thu hồi!",5)
set cx=null
set cC=null
return
endif
call pn(cV+1,GO)
if not ds(Bs[(cV+1)*5+GO]) then
call xx(Bs[(cV+1)*5+GO])
endif
call SaveInteger(cq,IA(CO,cV+1),LoadInteger(cq,IA(CQ,cV+1),GO),0)
call x7(cV+1,LoadInteger(cq,IA(CQ,cV+1),GO),-1)
set Bs[(cV+1)*5+GO]=null
call xF(cV+1,LoadInteger(cq,IA(CQ,cV+1),GO),1,false)
call SaveInteger(cq,IA(CQ,cV+1),GO,0)
call SaveReal(cq,IA(ws,cV+1),GO,0*1.)
call SaveReal(cq,IA(wU,cV+1),GO,0*1.)
endif
set NB[cV]=false
set cx=null
set cC=null
endfunction
function pW takes nothing returns nothing
local framehandle cx=BlzGetTriggerFrame()
local player cC=GetTriggerPlayer()
local integer cV=GetPlayerId(cC)
local integer GO=LoadInteger(es,Np,GetHandleId(cx))
local integer cz=LoadInteger(cq,IA(CQ,cV+1),GO)
local unit c4=Bs[(cV+1)*5+GO]
if cz!=0 then
if eB[cV+1] then
call xq(cV,0)
else
call xq(cV,1)
endif
call dg(cV,FX)
call aH(cV,Fq[1],"war3mapImported\\HeroIcon_"+I2S(cz)+".blp")
call dg(cV,Fq[1])
call dl(cV,F6[1],D3[LoadInteger(i9,DE[cz],Ds)]+" "+GetObjectName(DE[cz])+"\n"+C8[LoadInteger(i9,DE[cz],ey)]+BlzGetAbilityActivatedExtendedTooltip($41303237,cz-1))
call dl(cV,F6[3],BlzGetAbilityActivatedTooltip($41303237,cz-1))
set GP=iK+"HP: "+I2S(BlzGetUnitMaxHP(c4))+"|r -"+iO+" MP: "+I2S(BlzGetUnitMaxMana(c4))+"|r"
set GP=GP+"|nVật lý: Công "+I2S(xu(c4))+" - T"+LoadStr(kYb8,268784990,0)+"ủ "+I2S(R2I(LoadReal(eX,GetHandleId(c4),eq)*$64+.1))+"%"
set GP=GP+wF+"|nPhép: Công "+I2S(xb(c4))+" - T"+LoadStr(kYb8,268784990,0)+"ủ "+I2S(R2I(LoadReal(eX,GetHandleId(c4),e6)*$64+.1))+"%|r"
set GP=GP+NQ+"\nBạ"+LoadStr(kYb8,StringHash("Q7JR"),0)+": "+I2S(R2I(LoadReal(eX,GetHandleId(c4),eR))+10)+"% - Phá: "+I2S(R2I(LoadReal(eX,GetHandleId(c4),eK)))+"%|r -"+NX+" Đỡ: "+I2S(R2I(LoadReal(eX,GetHandleId(c4),er))+10)+"% - "+LoadStr(kYb8,StringHash("8fj{"),0)+"é: "+I2S(R2I(LoadReal(eX,GetHandleId(c4),ez))+10)+"%|r"
call dl(cV,F6[2],GP)
set GP=BlzGetAbilityActivatedTooltip($4D527872,cz-1)
if GP!="0" then
call dg(cV,Fq[2])
call aH(cV,Fq[2],"war3mapImported\\Skill"+I2S(cz)+"_1.blp")
call dl(cV,F6[4],GP)
endif
set GP=BlzGetAbilityActivatedExtendedTooltip($4D527872,cz-1)
if GP!="0" then
call dg(cV,Fq[3])
call aH(cV,Fq[3],"war3mapImported\\Skill"+I2S(cz)+"_2.blp")
call dl(cV,F6[5],GP)
endif
endif
set c4=null
set cx=null
set cC=null
endfunction

function ps takes integer cV,integer cp returns nothing
local real ij=-.02

local real Lx=0
if FQ[cV]!=cp then

if cp==1 then
set LV=.3
set Lx=-.1
endif
if GetLocalPlayer()==co[cV] then
call d0(FX,$12C,$1C0,.25+ij+LV,.27+Lx)
endif
if GetLocalPlayer()==co[cV] then
call d0(Fq[1],60,60,.37444+ij+LV,.47722+Lx)
endif
if GetLocalPlayer()==co[cV] then
call d0(Fq[2],40,40,.256+ij+LV,.40111+Lx)
endif
if GetLocalPlayer()==co[cV] then
call d0(Fq[3],40,40,.256+ij+LV,.36+Lx)
endif
if GetLocalPlayer()==co[cV] then
call BlzFrameSetAbsPoint(F6[1],FRAMEPOINT_TOPLEFT,.25388+ij+LV,.50722+Lx)
call BlzFrameSetAbsPoint(F6[1],FRAMEPOINT_BOTTOMRIGHT,.37955+ij+LV,.48277+Lx)
endif
if GetLocalPlayer()==co[cV] then
call BlzFrameSetAbsPoint(F6[2],FRAMEPOINT_TOPLEFT,.25388+ij+LV,.473+Lx)
call BlzFrameSetAbsPoint(F6[2],FRAMEPOINT_BOTTOMRIGHT,.41277+ij+LV,.433+Lx)
endif
if GetLocalPlayer()==co[cV] then
call BlzFrameSetAbsPoint(F6[3],FRAMEPOINT_TOPLEFT,.25944+ij+LV,.33388+Lx)
call BlzFrameSetAbsPoint(F6[3],FRAMEPOINT_BOTTOMRIGHT,.40777+ij+LV,.28166+Lx)
endif
if GetLocalPlayer()==co[cV] then
call BlzFrameSetAbsPoint(F6[4],FRAMEPOINT_TOPLEFT,.28+ij+LV,.43277+Lx)
call BlzFrameSetAbsPoint(F6[4],FRAMEPOINT_BOTTOMRIGHT,.41277+ij+LV,.39166+Lx)
endif
if GetLocalPlayer()==co[cV] then
call BlzFrameSetAbsPoint(F6[5],FRAMEPOINT_TOPLEFT,.28+ij+LV,.39166+Lx)
call BlzFrameSetAbsPoint(F6[5],FRAMEPOINT_BOTTOMRIGHT,.41277+ij+LV,.35055+Lx)

endif
endfunction

function pj takes integer c2,real ij returns nothing
set C0[c2]=df(" ")
call BlzFrameSetAbsPoint(C0[c2],FRAMEPOINT_TOPLEFT,ij,.04167+.021)
call BlzFrameSetAbsPoint(C0[c2],FRAMEPOINT_BOTTOMRIGHT,.06778+ij,.041)
call BlzFrameSetScale(C0[c2],1.3)
call BlzFrameSetTextAlignment(C0[c2],TEXT_JUSTIFY_TOP,TEXT_JUSTIFY_MIDDLE)
endfunction
function pJ takes integer c2,real ij returns nothing
set we[c2]=aj("war3mapImported\\TB_1_1_0.blp")
call d0(we[c2],40,40,ij-.00055,.01444)
call SaveInteger(es,Gu,GetHandleId(we[c2]),1)
call SaveInteger(es,Gk,GetHandleId(we[c2]),c2)
call aZ(we[c2],function xW)
call aE(we[c2],function xv)
call ah(we[c2],function xs)
set wn[c2]=aj("war3mapImported\\TB_1_2_0.blp")
call d0(wn[c2],40,40,ij+.02222,.01444)
call SaveInteger(es,Gu,GetHandleId(wn[c2]),2)
call SaveInteger(es,Gk,GetHandleId(wn[c2]),c2)
call aZ(wn[c2],function xW)
call aE(wn[c2],function xv)
call ah(wn[c2],function xs)
set wW[c2]=aj("war3mapImported\\TB_1_3_0.blp")
call d0(wW[c2],40,40,ij+.04499,.01444)
call SaveInteger(es,Gu,GetHandleId(wW[c2]),3)
call SaveInteger(es,Gk,GetHandleId(wW[c2]),c2)
call aZ(wW[c2],function xW)
call aE(wW[c2],function xv)
call ah(wW[c2],function xs)
endfunction
function p9 takes integer c2,unit c4,integer GO returns nothing
if not ds(c4) then
if IsUnitSelected(c4,co[c2-1]) then
if GetLocalPlayer()==co[c2-1] then
call PanCameraToTimed(GetUnitX(c4),GetUnitY(c4),0.)
endif
endif
if GetLocalPlayer()==co[c2-1] then
call ClearSelection()
call SelectUnit(c4,true)
endif
elseif GO!=0 then
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(CQ,c2),GO)==0 and Fu[c2-1]!=0 then
call x3(c2,Fu[c2-1],GO)
endif
endif
endfunction
function p3 takes nothing returns nothing
local player cC=GetTriggerPlayer()
local integer c2=GetPlayerId(cC)+1
local oskeytype Nq=BlzGetTriggerPlayerKey()
if Nq==OSKEY_1 then
call p9(c2,Bs[c2*5+1],1)
endif
if Nq==OSKEY_2 then
call p9(c2,Bs[c2*5+2],2)
endif
if Nq==OSKEY_3 then
call p9(c2,Bs[c2*5+3],3)
endif
if Nq==OSKEY_4 then
call p9(c2,Bs[c2*5+4],4)
endif
if Nq==OSKEY_5 then
call p9(c2,Bs[c2*5+5],5)
endif
set Nq=null
set cC=null
endfunction
function ph takes nothing returns nothing
call fx()
call pU()
call pH(1,.38778)
call pH(2,.46777)
call pH(3,.54778)
call pH(4,.62775)
call pH(5,.70778)
call pB(1,.40222)
call pB(2,.48222)
call pB(3,.56222)
call pB(4,.64223)
call pB(5,.72222)
call pj(1,.39055)
call pj(2,.47055)
call pj(3,.55055)
call pj(4,.63055)
call pj(5,.71055)
call pJ(1,.39111)
call pJ(2,.47111)
call pJ(3,.55111)
call pJ(4,.63111)
call pJ(5,.71111)
call fK(OSKEY_1,function p3)
call fK(OSKEY_2,function p3)
call fK(OSKEY_3,function p3)
call fK(OSKEY_4,function p3)
call fK(OSKEY_5,function p3)
endfunction
function pZ takes nothing returns nothing
set CQ=I4()
set Fe=I4()
call fM(2.,function ph)
endfunction
function pE takes integer c2,integer cp,integer e3,integer eh,integer eZ returns boolean
if e3==eh then
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(ee,c2),e3)>=2 and LoadInteger(cq,IA(ee,c2),eZ)>=1 then
call xF(c2,e3,-2,true)
call xF(c2,eZ,-1,true)
return true
endif
else
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(ee,c2),e3)>=1 and LoadInteger(cq,IA(ee,c2),eh)>=1 and LoadInteger(cq,IA(ee,c2),eZ)>=1 then
call xF(c2,e3,-1,true)
call xF(c2,eh,-1,true)
call xF(c2,eZ,-1,true)
return true
endif
endif
return false
endfunction
function pA takes nothing returns nothing
local framehandle cx=BlzGetTriggerFrame()
local player cC=GetTriggerPlayer()
local integer cV=GetPlayerId(cC)
local integer GO=LoadInteger(es,N6,GetHandleId(cx))
if NB[cV] then
set cx=null
set cC=null
return
endif
if GetLocalPlayer()==cC then
call BlzFrameSetEnable(cx,false)
call BlzFrameSetEnable(cx,true)
endif
if pE(cV+1,GO,LoadInteger(e5,GO,eT),LoadInteger(e5,GO,eC),LoadInteger(e5,GO,e4)) then
call xF(cV+1,GO,1,true)
endif
set cx=null
set cC=null
endfunction
function pD takes nothing returns nothing
local integer cV=GetPlayerId(GetTriggerPlayer())
local integer GO=Fu[cV]
if BlzGetTriggerPlayerMouseButton()==MOUSE_BUTTON_TYPE_RIGHT and eB[cV+1] and GO>0 then
set Na=x9(cV+1)
if Na!=0 then
call x3(cV+1,GO,Na)
endif
endif
endfunction
function p5 takes nothing returns nothing
local framehandle cx=BlzGetTriggerFrame()
local player cC=GetTriggerPlayer()
local integer cV=GetPlayerId(cC)
local integer GO=LoadInteger(es,N6,GetHandleId(cx))
if GetLocalPlayer()==cC then
call BlzFrameSetEnable(cx,false)
call BlzFrameSetEnable(cx,true)
endif
if NB[cV] then
if GO>8 then
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(ee,cV+1),GO)>0 then
call xF(cV+1,GO,-1,true)
call xY(cV+1,Nz[GO])
call dc(cC,"Đã "+LoadStr(kYb8,1411306174,0)+LoadStr(kYb8,377277493,0)+LoadStr(kYb8,StringHash("KiJk"),0)+" "+GetObjectName(DE[GO])+" đư"+LoadStr(kYb8,-2012720143,0)+"c "+I2S(Nz[GO])+" đạo duyên.",5)
endif
else
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(ee,cV+1),GO)>=2 then
call xF(cV+1,GO,-2,true)
call xY(cV+1,1)
call dc(cC,"Đã bán 2 "+GetObjectName(DE[GO])+" được 1 đạo duyên.",5)
else
call dc(cC,ir+"Mỗi lần bán cần 2 nhân vật cấp [Linh].|r",5)
endif
endif
else
if Fk[cV+1]!=GO then
call dP(cV,Fb[Fk[cV+1]])
set Fk[cV+1]=GO
call dg(cV,Fb[GO])
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(Fd,cV+1),1)!=0 then
call dP(cV,Ff[LoadInteger(cq,IA(Fd,cV+1),1)])
call SaveInteger(cq,IA(Fd,cV+1),1,0)
endif
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(Fd,cV+1),2)!=0 then
call dP(cV,Ff[LoadInteger(cq,IA(Fd,cV+1),2)])
call SaveInteger(cq,IA(Fd,cV+1),2,0)
endif
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(Fd,cV+1),3)!=0 then
call dP(cV,Ff[LoadInteger(cq,IA(Fd,cV+1),3)])
call SaveInteger(cq,IA(Fd,cV+1),3,0)
endif
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(Fd,cV+1),4)!=0 then
call dP(cV,Ff[LoadInteger(cq,IA(Fd,cV+1),4)])
call SaveInteger(cq,IA(Fd,cV+1),4,0)
endif
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(Fd,cV+1),5)!=0 then
call dP(cV,Ff[LoadInteger(cq,IA(Fd,cV+1),5)])
call SaveInteger(cq,IA(Fd,cV+1),5,0)
endif
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(Fd,cV+1),6)!=0 then
call dP(cV,Fa[LoadInteger(cq,IA(Fd,cV+1),6)])
call SaveInteger(cq,IA(Fd,cV+1),6,0)
endif
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(Fd,cV+1),7)!=0 then
call dP(cV,Fa[LoadInteger(cq,IA(Fd,cV+1),7)])
call SaveInteger(cq,IA(Fd,cV+1),7,0)
endif
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(Fd,cV+1),8)!=0 then
call dP(cV,Fa[LoadInteger(cq,IA(Fd,cV+1),8)])
call SaveInteger(cq,IA(Fd,cV+1),8,0)
endif
call SaveInteger(cq,IA(Fd,cV+1),1,LoadInteger(e5,GO,ew))
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(Fd,cV+1),1)>0 then
call dg(cV,Ff[LoadInteger(cq,IA(Fd,cV+1),1)])
endif
call SaveInteger(cq,IA(Fd,cV+1),2,LoadInteger(e5,GO,eG))
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(Fd,cV+1),2)>0 then
call dg(cV,Ff[LoadInteger(cq,IA(Fd,cV+1),2)])
endif
call SaveInteger(cq,IA(Fd,cV+1),3,LoadInteger(e5,GO,eF))
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(Fd,cV+1),3)>0 then
call dg(cV,Ff[LoadInteger(cq,IA(Fd,cV+1),3)])
endif
call SaveInteger(cq,IA(Fd,cV+1),4,LoadInteger(e5,GO,eN))
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(Fd,cV+1),4)>0 then
call dg(cV,Ff[LoadInteger(cq,IA(Fd,cV+1),4)])
endif
call SaveInteger(cq,IA(Fd,cV+1),5,LoadInteger(e5,GO,e2))
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(Fd,cV+1),5)>0 then
call dg(cV,Ff[LoadInteger(cq,IA(Fd,cV+1),5)])
endif
if GO>8 then
call SaveInteger(cq,IA(Fd,cV+1),6,LoadInteger(e5,GO,eT))
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(Fd,cV+1),6)>0 then
call dg(cV,Fa[LoadInteger(cq,IA(Fd,cV+1),6)])
endif
call SaveInteger(cq,IA(Fd,cV+1),7,LoadInteger(e5,GO,eC))
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(Fd,cV+1),7)>0 then
call dg(cV,Fa[LoadInteger(cq,IA(Fd,cV+1),7)])
endif
call SaveInteger(cq,IA(Fd,cV+1),8,LoadInteger(e5,GO,e4))
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(Fd,cV+1),8)>0 then
call dg(cV,Fa[LoadInteger(cq,IA(Fd,cV+1),8)])
endif
endif
endif
endif
set NB[cV]=false
set cx=null
set cC=null
endfunction
function pT takes nothing returns nothing
local framehandle cx=BlzGetTriggerFrame()
local player cC=GetTriggerPlayer()
local integer cV=GetPlayerId(cC)
local integer GO=LoadInteger(es,N6,GetHandleId(cx))
call ps(cV,0)
call dg(cV,FX)
call aH(cV,Fq[1],"war3mapImported\\HeroIcon_"+I2S(GO)+".blp")
call dg(cV,Fq[1])
call dl(cV,F6[1],D3[LoadInteger(i9,DE[GO],Ds)]+" "+GetObjectName(DE[GO])+"\n"+C8[LoadInteger(i9,DE[GO],ey)]+BlzGetAbilityActivatedExtendedTooltip($41303237,GO-1))
call dl(cV,F6[2],BlzGetAbilityTooltip($4D527872,GO-1))
if GO>8 then
call dl(cV,F6[3],BlzGetAbilityExtendedTooltip($4D527872,GO-1))
endif
set GP=BlzGetAbilityActivatedTooltip($4D527872,GO-1)
if GP!="0" then
call dg(cV,Fq[2])
call aH(cV,Fq[2],"war3mapImported\\Skill"+I2S(GO)+"_1.blp")
call dl(cV,F6[4],GP)
endif
set GP=BlzGetAbilityActivatedExtendedTooltip($4D527872,GO-1)
if GP!="0" then
call dg(cV,Fq[3])
call aH(cV,Fq[3],"war3mapImported\\Skill"+I2S(GO)+"_2.blp")
call dl(cV,F6[5],GP)
endif
set Fu[cV]=GO
set cx=null
set cC=null
endfunction

endfunction
function p4 takes integer c2,real ij,real iJ returns nothing
set eH[c2]=dO("war3mapImported\\DisHeroIcon_"+I2S(c2)+".blp",5)

call BlzFrameSetScale(ej[c2],1.3)
call BlzFrameSetTextAlignment(ej[c2],TEXT_JUSTIFY_TOP,TEXT_JUSTIFY_LEFT)
set FI[c2]=aj(NY)

call BlzFrameSetVisible(FI[c2],false)
call aZ(FI[c2],function pT)
call aE(FI[c2],function pC)

call BlzFrameSetVisible(eD[c2],false)

call d0(Ff[c2],58,58,ij-.00055,iJ-.00055)
call BlzFrameSetVisible(Ff[c2],false)

endfunction

function pG takes nothing returns nothing
local player cC=GetTriggerPlayer()
local integer c2=GetPlayerId(cC)+1
local integer cV=c2-1
local integer cp=1
if not eB[c2] then
set eB[c2]=true
set Fk[c2]=0
loop
exitwhen cp>70
call dg(c2-1,eH[cp])
call dg(c2-1,FI[cp])
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(ee,c2),cp)>0 then
call dl(c2-1,ej[cp],"x"+I2S(LoadInteger(cq,IA(ee,c2),cp)))
endif
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(eE,c2),cp)==1 then
call dg(c2-1,eA[cp])
call dg(c2-1,eD[cp])
endif
set cp=cp+1
endloop
call xf(cV)
else
call xp(cC,c2)
endif
set cC=null
endfunction
function pF takes nothing returns nothing
local player cC=GetTriggerPlayer()
local integer c2=GetPlayerId(cC)+1
local integer GO=0
call xp(cC,c2)
call xe(cC,c2)
if Nc[c2] then
set Nc[c2]=false
call dP(c2-1,F8)
endif
set GO=9
if Nr[c2] then
set Nr[c2]=false
loop
exitwhen GO>18
call dP(c2-1,ck[GO])
set GO=GO+1
endloop
call dP(c2-1,ck[57])
call dP(c2-1,ck[58])
endif
set cC=null
endfunction

function pY takes nothing returns nothing
set Fd=I4()
set eE=I4()
set FT=I4()
call fM(3.,function pw)
call fK(OSKEY_B,function pG)
call fr(function pF)
call fM(3.555,function p2)

endfunction
function pP takes nothing returns nothing
local integer c6=ay()
set Fo[c6]=Fo[c6]+1
if eB[FV[c6]+1] then
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(eE,FV[c6]+1),Fo[c6])==1 then
if GetLocalPlayer()==co[FV[c6]] then
call BlzFrameSetAlpha(eD[Fo[c6]],Fx[c6])
endif
endif
else
call a0()
call xa(c6)
return
endif
if Fo[c6]==70 then
set Fo[c6]=0
if Fx[c6]==$78 then
set Fx[c6]=$FF
else
set Fx[c6]=$78
endif
endif
endfunction
function pm takes unit c4 returns nothing
if c4!=null then
call FlushChildHashtable(ef,GetHandleId(c4))
call FlushChildHashtable(eX,GetHandleId(c4))
call RemoveUnit(c4)
endif
endfunction
function po takes unit c4,real cm returns nothing
if LoadInteger(i9,GetUnitTypeId(c4),iA)==0 then
call BlzSetUnitBaseDamage(c4,BlzGetUnitBaseDamage(c4,0)+R2I(cm),0)

endfunction

endfunction

function pk takes unit c4,real cm returns boolean
if GetUnitState(c4,UNIT_STATE_MANA)>=cm then
call xl(c4,-cm)
return true
endif
return false
endfunction
function pI takes unit c4,real iQ returns nothing
local real iX=GetUnitState(c4,UNIT_STATE_LIFE)
if iQ<0 then
if LoadInteger(eX,GetHandleId(c4),i6)==0 or GetUnitAbilityLevel(c4,$42303051)>0 and not (GetUnitAbilityLevel(c4,$4E506A73)>0) then
if -1*iQ>=iX then
set iQ=-1*(iX-1)
endif
if -1*iQ>1 then
call dL(co[dv(c4)-1],ir+I2S(R2I(iQ)),GetUnitX(c4),GetUnitY(c4),11,false)
call SetUnitState(c4,UNIT_STATE_LIFE,iX+iQ)
endif
endif
else
if iQ!=0 then
if GetUnitAbilityLevel(c4,$42303059)>0 then
return
endif
call dL(co[dv(c4)-1],iK++I2S(R2I(iQ)),GetUnitX(c4),GetUnitY(c4),11,true)
call SetUnitState(c4,UNIT_STATE_LIFE,iX+iQ)
endif
endif
endfunction
function pf takes unit c4,real iQ returns nothing
if iQ<0 then
if LoadInteger(eX,GetHandleId(c4),iR)==0 or GetUnitAbilityLevel(c4,$42303051)>0 then
call dL(co[dv(c4)-1],iO+I2S(R2I(iQ)),GetUnitX(c4),GetUnitY(c4),11,false)
call SetUnitState(c4,UNIT_STATE_MANA,GetUnitState(c4,UNIT_STATE_MANA)+iQ)
endif
else
if GetUnitAbilityLevel(c4,$42303059)>0 then
return
endif
call dL(co[dv(c4)-1],iO++I2S(R2I(iQ)),GetUnitX(c4),GetUnitY(c4),11,true)
call SetUnitState(c4,UNIT_STATE_MANA,GetUnitState(c4,UNIT_STATE_MANA)+iQ)
endif
endfunction
function pd takes unit c4,real iQ returns nothing
local real iX=GetUnitState(c4,UNIT_STATE_LIFE)
set iq=iQ*GetUnitState(c4,UNIT_STATE_MAX_LIFE)
if iQ<0 then
if LoadInteger(eX,GetHandleId(c4),i6)==0 and not IsUnitType(c4,UNIT_TYPE_TAUREN) and not (GetUnitAbilityLevel(c4,$4E506A73)>0) then
if -1*iq>=iX then
set iq=-1*(iX-1)
endif
if -1*iq>1 then
call dL(co[dv(c4)-1],ir+I2S(R2I(iq)),GetUnitX(c4),GetUnitY(c4),11,false)
call SetUnitState(c4,UNIT_STATE_LIFE,iX+iq)
endif
endif
else
if iq!=0 then
if GetUnitAbilityLevel(c4,$42303059)>0 then
return
endif
call dL(co[dv(c4)-1],iK++I2S(R2I(iq)),GetUnitX(c4),GetUnitY(c4),11,true)
call SetUnitState(c4,UNIT_STATE_LIFE,iX+iq)
endif
endif
endfunction

endfunction

call p0(60,15,18,57)
call p0(61,14,17,58)
call p0(27,25,19,17)
call p0(28,26,59,12)
call p0(29,23,22,57)
call p0(30,24,25,9)
call p0(31,24,26,18)
call p0(32,19,19,2)
call p0(33,22,22,6)
call p0(34,20,23,11)
call p0(35,21,26,14)
call p0(36,20,60,13)
call p0(62,9,19,61)
call p0(63,20,20,57)
call p0(64,16,21,59)
call p0(37,30,31,23)
call p0(38,27,27,21)
call p0(39,28,28,21)
call p0(40,32,32,15)
call p0(41,36,34,59)
call p0(42,30,29,26)
call p0(43,35,31,20)
call p0(44,36,33,24)
call p0(45,29,28,61)
call p0(46,33,35,22)
call p0(65,35,63,60)
call p0(66,25,33,64)
call p0(67,61,27,34)
call p0(47,37,38,34)
call p0(48,41,44,31)
call p0(49,46,43,32)
call p0(50,41,44,35)
call p0(51,38,42,36)
call p0(52,39,45,30)
call p0(53,42,37,62)
call p0(54,40,40,25)
call p0(55,46,65,27)
call p0(56,39,45,64)
call p0(68,37,67,62)
call p0(69,43,66,63)
call p0(70,33,42,65)
endfunction
function Qc takes nothing returns nothing
set ee=I4()
set G2=I4()
call fM(2.,function py)
call fM(4.,function p8)
call fM(3.,function pO)
endfunction
function Q7 takes unit c4 returns damagetype
if GetUnitAbilityLevel(c4,nD)>0 then
return n5
elseif GetUnitAbilityLevel(c4,nT)>0 then
return nC
elseif GetUnitAbilityLevel(c4,n4)>0 then
return nw
elseif GetUnitAbilityLevel(c4,nG)>0 then
return nF
else
return nN
endif
endfunction
function Q1 takes unit c4,unit cP,integer nZ,real nE returns nothing
if nZ==nA then
call UnitDamageTarget(c4,cP,nE,true,true,ATTACK_TYPE_MAGIC,Q7(c4),null)
else
call UnitDamageTarget(c4,cP,nE,true,true,ATTACK_TYPE_HERO,Q7(c4),null)
endif
endfunction
function Qe takes integer c6 returns nothing
if YE[c6]>0 then
if LoadInteger(eX,GetHandleId(Yg[c6]),iA)==1 then
set YA[c6]=true
endif
if GetUnitAbilityLevel(Yg[c6],nD)>0 then
set YP[c6]=n5
elseif GetUnitAbilityLevel(Yg[c6],n4)>0 then
set YP[c6]=nw
elseif GetUnitAbilityLevel(Yg[c6],nG)>0 then
set YP[c6]=nF
elseif GetUnitAbilityLevel(Yg[c6],nT)>0 then
set YP[c6]=nC
else
set YP[c6]=nN
endif
endif
endfunction
function Qi takes integer c6 returns nothing
local real Yx=1.
if YE[c6]>0 then
if YP[c6]==nN then
if YV[c6]==n5 then
set Yx=1.25
elseif YV[c6]==nF then
set Yx=.75
endif
elseif YP[c6]==n5 then
if YV[c6]==nC then
set Yx=1.25
elseif YV[c6]==nN then
set Yx=.75
endif
elseif YP[c6]==nw then
if YV[c6]==nF then
set Yx=1.25
elseif YV[c6]==nC then
set Yx=.75
endif
elseif YP[c6]==nF then
if YV[c6]==nN then
set Yx=1.25
elseif YV[c6]==nw then
set Yx=.75
endif
elseif YP[c6]==nC then
if YV[c6]==nw then
set Yx=1.25
elseif YV[c6]==n5 then
set Yx=.75
endif
endif
set YE[c6]=YE[c6]*Yx
endif
endfunction
function Qn takes integer c6 returns nothing
set YT[c6]=false
set Y5[c6]=false
set YC[c6]=0.
set Y4[c6]=0.
set Yw[c6]=0.
if jI then
set YD[c6]=true
set jI=false
endif
if vs then
set Y5[c6]=true
set vs=false
endif
if ZV then
set YT[c6]=true
set ZV=false
endif
if jf!=0. then
set YC[c6]=jf
set jf=0.
endif
if vU!=0. then
set Y4[c6]=vU
set vU=0.
endif
if Br>0. then
set Yw[c6]=Br
set Br=0.
endif
endfunction
function QL takes integer c6 returns boolean
local real Yl=10+LoadReal(eX,GetHandleId(Yg[c6]),eR)-LoadReal(eX,GetHandleId(Yt[c6]),iZ)
if GetRandomReal(0,$64)<Yl+Y4[c6] then
if GetUnitAbilityLevel(Yg[c6],$42303042)>0 then
set YC[c6]=YC[c6]+.2
endif
return true
endif
return false
endfunction
function QW takes integer c6 returns boolean
local real Yu=10+LoadReal(eX,GetHandleId(Yt[c6]),ez)-LoadReal(eX,GetHandleId(Yg[c6]),eO)
if Yu>90 then
set Yu=90
endif
if GetRandomReal(0,$64)<Yu then
return true
endif
return false
endfunction
function Qv takes integer c6 returns boolean
local real Yb=10+LoadReal(eX,GetHandleId(Yt[c6]),er)-LoadReal(eX,GetHandleId(Yg[c6]),eK)
if GetRandomReal(0,$64)<Yb then
return true
endif
return false
endfunction
function Qs takes integer c6 returns nothing
local integer Yx=0
local real Yk=0
if YE[c6]>0. then
if GetUnitAbilityLevel(Yt[c6],$41303339)>0 then
call pI(Yg[c6],-140)
endif
if GetUnitAbilityLevel(Yt[c6],$4D707877)>0 then
call VH(Yg[c6],5.,25)
endif
if GetUnitAbilityLevel(Yt[c6],$41303332)>0 then
if p6(Yt[c6])<=35 and not vj[YZ[c6]] and GetUnitState(Yt[c6],UNIT_STATE_MANA)>=$64 then
set YE[c6]=0
call gB(Yt[c6],YZ[c6])
endif
endif
if GetUnitAbilityLevel(Yt[c6],$41303334)>0 and not Wh[YZ[c6]] then
set Yk=GetUnitState(Yt[c6],UNIT_STATE_LIFE)
if Yk+YE[c6]<=GetUnitState(Yt[c6],UNIT_STATE_MAX_LIFE)*.15 and GetUnitState(Yt[c6],UNIT_STATE_MANA)>=$C8 then
set YE[c6]=0
call PR(Yt[c6])
endif
endif
if GetUnitAbilityLevel(Yt[c6],Z8)>0 then
set Yx=LoadInteger(eX,GetHandleId(Yt[c6]),Z0)
if Yx>0 then
set YE[c6]=0
set Yx=Yx-1
call SaveInteger(eX,GetHandleId(Yt[c6]),Z0,Yx)
if Yx==0 then
call dJ(Yt[c6],Z8,$42303046)
endif
endif
endif
if GetUnitAbilityLevel(Yt[c6],$41303239)>0 and LoadInteger(cq,IA(WE,YZ[c6]),12)>=2 then
if p6(Yt[c6])<=50 then
call t4(Yt[c6],YZ[c6])
endif
endif
if GetUnitAbilityLevel(Yt[c6],$41303248)>0 then
set YE[c6]=0
call dJ(Yt[c6],$41303248,$42303054)
endif
set Yk=LoadReal(eX,GetHandleId(Yg[c6]),B9)
if Yk>0 then
set YE[c6]=YE[c6]*(1.+Yk)
endif
set Yk=LoadReal(eX,GetHandleId(Yt[c6]),vA)
if Yk>0 then
if Yk>.99 then
set Yk=.99
endif
set YE[c6]=YE[c6]*(1.-Yk)
endif
if GetUnitAbilityLevel(Yg[c6],$42303051)>0 and GetRandomReal(0,$64)<=15 then
set Yw[c6]=Yw[c6]+.7
endif
endif
endfunction
function QU takes integer c6,unit c4,unit cP returns nothing
local integer cV=GetUnitTypeId(c4)
if GetUnitAbilityLevel(c4,$41303241)>0 or GetUnitAbilityLevel(c4,$41303242)>0 or GetUnitAbilityLevel(c4,$41303245)>0 then
set YE[c6]=0
call P6(c4,cP)
else
if cV==DE[6] then
set YE[c6]=0
call DestroyEffect(AddSpecialEffect("war3mapImported\\Effect_loichu.mdl",GetUnitX(cP)*1.,GetUnitY(cP)*1.))
call V7(c4,cP,$78)
endif
if cV==DE[18] then
set YE[c6]=0
call V7(c4,cP,$96)
endif
if cV==DE[35] then
set YE[c6]=0
call m4(c4,cP)
endif
if cV==DE[47] then
set YE[c6]=0
call tt(c4,cP)
endif
if cV==DE[49] then
set YE[c6]=0
call t5(c4,cP)
endif
if cV==DE[70] then
set YE[c6]=0
call gc(c4,cP)
endif
if cV==DE[65] then
call g2(c4,cP)
endif
if cV==DE[29] then
call DestroyEffect(AddSpecialEffect("war3mapImported\\Effect_loichu.mdl",GetUnitX(cP)*1.,GetUnitY(cP)*1.))
endif
if cV==DE[34] then
set YE[c6]=0
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(WE,Yh[c6]),3)>=2 then
call mF(c4,cP,5)
else
call mF(c4,cP,3)
endif
endif
if cV==DE[25] and LoadInteger(cq,IA(WE,Yh[c6]),1)>=2 then
set YE[c6]=0
call oD(c4)
endif
if cV==DE[43] then
set YE[c6]=0
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(WE,Yh[c6]),7)>=2 then
call tz(c4,cP,3)
else
call tz(c4,cP,1)
endif
endif
if cV==DE[22] then
set YE[c6]=0
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(WE,Yh[c6]),17)>=2 then
call og(c4,cP,6)
else
call og(c4,cP,3)
endif
endif
if cV==DE[46] then
set YE[c6]=0
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(WE,Yh[c6]),11)>=2 then
call tV(c4,cP,3)
else
call tV(c4,cP,1)
endif
endif
if cV==DE[30] or cV==DE[31] then
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(WE,Yh[c6]),8)>=2 and GetRandomReal(0,$64)<=35 then
call mR(c4,cP)
endif
if cV==DE[31] and LoadInteger(cq,IA(WE,Yh[c6]),2)>=2 then
set YE[c6]=0
call mz(c4,cP)
endif
endif
if cV==DE[54] then
call DestroyEffect(AddSpecialEffectTarget("war3mapImported\\Effect_khoaphutarget.mdl",cP,"chest"))
call pd(c4,.06)
endif
endif
endfunction
function QH takes nothing returns nothing
local integer c6=xm()
local boolean Ym=false
local real Yo=0
call DisableTrigger(GetTriggeringTrigger())
set YP[c6]=BlzGetEventDamageType()
set YA[c6]=BlzGetEventAttackType()==ATTACK_TYPE_MAGIC
set Yg[c6]=GetEventDamageSource()
set Yt[c6]=BlzGetEventDamageTarget()
set Yh[c6]=dv(Yg[c6])
set YZ[c6]=dv(Yt[c6])
set YE[c6]=GetEventDamage()
call Qn(c6)
if YP[c6]==DAMAGE_TYPE_NORMAL then
set Ym=true
call QU(c6,Yg[c6],Yt[c6])
call Qe(c6)
endif
if GetUnitAbilityLevel(Yt[c6],$4E4D6556)>0 then
set YE[c6]=0
endif
if YE[c6]==0 or ds(Yt[c6]) then
call BlzSetEventDamage(0.)
set Y2[c6]=null
set Yg[c6]=null
set Yt[c6]=null
call xo(c6)
call EnableTrigger(GetTriggeringTrigger())
return
endif
if YA[c6]==false then
set Yo=LoadReal(eX,GetHandleId(Yt[c6]),eq)
if GetUnitAbilityLevel(Yt[c6],$42303052)>0 then
set YE[c6]=YE[c6]*1.2
endif
else
set Yo=LoadReal(eX,GetHandleId(Yt[c6]),e6)
if GetUnitAbilityLevel(Yt[c6],hU)>0 then
set YE[c6]=YE[c6]*1.2
endif
endif
if Yo>1. then
set Yo=1.
endif
set YE[c6]=YE[c6]*(1.-Yo)
call Qi(c6)
if YT[c6]==false and YE[c6]>0 then
if QL(c6) or YD[c6] then
set YF[c6]=true
set YE[c6]=YE[c6]*(YC[c6]+LoadReal(eX,GetHandleId(Yg[c6]),ih)+1.5)
endif
endif
if Y5[c6]==false and YE[c6]>0 then
if QW(c6) then
set YE[c6]=0.
set YG[c6]=true
endif
endif
if Qv(c6) and YE[c6]>0 then
set YE[c6]=YE[c6]*.5
set YN[c6]=true
endif
call Qs(c6)
if YE[c6]<=0. then
set YE[c6]=0.
endif
if YE[c6]>0. and Yw[c6]>0. then
call DestroyEffect(AddSpecialEffectTarget("Abilities\\Spells\\Undead\\VampiricAura\\VampiricAuraTarget.mdl",Yg[c6],"origin"))
call pI(Yg[c6],Yw[c6]*YE[c6])
endif
if not de(Yg[c6]) then
if YG[c6]==true then
set Y2[c6]="|cff6d6d6dTrượt|r"
set YY[c6]=10
elseif YE[c6]<=0 then
set Y2[c6]="|cff6d6d6dThủ|r"
set YY[c6]=10
else
if YF[c6] then
set Y2[c6]=I2S(R2I(YE[c6]))
set YY[c6]=14
elseif YN[c6] then
set Y2[c6]=NX+"Đỡ "+I2S(R2I(YE[c6]))+"|r"
set YY[c6]=11
else
if YA[c6] then
set Y2[c6]=NQ+I2S(R2I(YE[c6]))+"|r"
else
set Y2[c6]=I2S(R2I(YE[c6]))
endif
set YY[c6]=11
endif
endif
if co[Yh[c6]-1]!=eu then
if YF[c6] and YG[c6]==false then
call d4(co[Yh[c6]-1],Y2[c6],GetUnitX(Yt[c6]),GetUnitY(Yt[c6]))
else
if GetLocalPlayer()==co[Yh[c6]-1] then
call dn(Y2[c6],GetUnitX(Yt[c6]),GetUnitY(Yt[c6]),YY[c6])
endif
endif
endif
if co[YZ[c6]-1]!=eu then
if YE[c6]<=0 or YG[c6]==true or Ym==false then
if YF[c6] and YG[c6]==false and YE[c6]>0 then
call d4(co[YZ[c6]-1],Y2[c6],GetUnitX(Yt[c6]),GetUnitY(Yt[c6]))
else
if GetLocalPlayer()==co[YZ[c6]-1] then
call dn(Y2[c6],GetUnitX(Yt[c6]),GetUnitY(Yt[c6]),YY[c6])
endif
endif
endif
endif
endif
call BlzSetEventDamage(YE[c6])
set Y2[c6]=null
set Yg[c6]=null
set Yt[c6]=null
call xo(c6)
call EnableTrigger(GetTriggeringTrigger())
endfunction
function QB takes nothing returns nothing
call fp(EVENT_PLAYER_UNIT_DAMAGED,function QH)
endfunction
function Qj takes integer c2,integer cP returns nothing
set cg[c2]=cg[c2]+cP
if cP>0 then
call dc(co[c2-1],"|cff0fdfffNhận được "+I2S(cP)+" Đạo Duyên|r",5)
endif
call dl(c2-1,cu,"Đạo Duyên: "+I2S(cg[c2]-cb))
if cg[c2]>=cb+5 then
call dg(c2-1,ck[1])
call dl(c2-1,cI[1],""+LoadStr(kYb8,StringHash("d9kR"),0)+" Đ"+LoadStr(kYb8,StringHash("Lhio"),0)+"")
if cf>=15 then
call dg(c2-1,ck[2])
call dl(c2-1,cI[2],"3 Đ"+LoadStr(kYb8,StringHash("Lhio"),0)+"")
endif
if cf>=30 then
call dg(c2-1,ck[3])
call dl(c2-1,cI[3],"5 Đ"+LoadStr(kYb8,StringHash("Lhio"),0)+"")
endif
elseif cg[c2]>=cb+3 then
call dg(c2-1,ck[1])
call dl(c2-1,cI[1],""+LoadStr(kYb8,StringHash("d9kR"),0)+" Đ"+LoadStr(kYb8,StringHash("Lhio"),0)+"")
if cf>=15 then
call dg(c2-1,ck[2])
call dl(c2-1,cI[2],"3 Đ"+LoadStr(kYb8,StringHash("Lhio"),0)+"")
endif
elseif cg[c2]>=cb+1 then
call dg(c2-1,ck[1])
call dl(c2-1,cI[1],""+LoadStr(kYb8,StringHash("d9kR"),0)+" Đ"+LoadStr(kYb8,StringHash("Lhio"),0)+"")
endif
endfunction
function Q9 takes integer c2,integer cP returns nothing
set iL[c2]=iL[c2]+cP
call dl(c2-1,iW,ic+"Linh Thạch: "+I2S(iL[c2]-cb)+"|r")
endfunction
function Qh takes integer c2,integer cp,integer e3,integer eh,integer eZ returns nothing
if e3==eh then
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(ee,c2),e3)>=2 and LoadInteger(cq,IA(ee,c2),eZ)>=1 then
call SaveInteger(cq,IA(eE,c2),cp,1)
if eB[c2] then
call dg(c2-1,eA[cp])
call dg(c2-1,eD[cp])
endif
else
call SaveInteger(cq,IA(eE,c2),cp,0)
if eB[c2] then
call dP(c2-1,eA[cp])
call dP(c2-1,eD[cp])
endif
endif
else
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(ee,c2),e3)>=1 and LoadInteger(cq,IA(ee,c2),eh)>=1 and LoadInteger(cq,IA(ee,c2),eZ)>=1 then
call SaveInteger(cq,IA(eE,c2),cp,1)
if eB[c2] then
call dg(c2-1,eA[cp])
call dg(c2-1,eD[cp])
endif
else
call SaveInteger(cq,IA(eE,c2),cp,0)
if eB[c2] then
call dP(c2-1,eA[cp])
call dP(c2-1,eD[cp])
endif
endif
endif
endfunction
function QZ takes integer c2,integer cp returns nothing
if cp!=0 then
call Qh(c2,cp,LoadInteger(e5,cp,eT),LoadInteger(e5,cp,eC),LoadInteger(e5,cp,e4))
endif
endfunction
function QE takes nothing returns integer
local integer cp=GetRandomInt(1,20)
if cp>18 then
set cp=cp-18+56
endif
return cp
endfunction
function QA takes nothing returns integer
local integer cp=GetRandomInt(19,29)
if cp>26 then
set cp=cp-26+58
endif
return cp
endfunction
function QD takes integer c2,integer cp,integer e7,boolean e1 returns nothing
call SaveInteger(cq,IA(ee,c2),cp,LoadInteger(cq,IA(ee,c2),cp)+e7)
if not ei[c2] then
call x5(c2)
endif
if e7>0 then
call aH(c2-1,eH[cp],"war3mapImported\\HeroIcon_"+I2S(cp)+".blp")
if eB[c2] then
call dl(c2-1,ej[cp],"x"+I2S(LoadInteger(cq,IA(ee,c2),cp)))
endif
if e1 then
call x4(c2,cp)
endif
else
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(ee,c2),cp)==0 then
call aH(c2-1,eH[cp],"war3mapImported\\DisHeroIcon_"+I2S(cp)+".blp")
call dl(c2-1,ej[cp]," ")
else
if eB[c2] then
call dl(c2-1,ej[cp],"x"+I2S(LoadInteger(cq,IA(ee,c2),cp)))
endif
endif
endif
call QZ(c2,LoadInteger(e5,cp,ew))
call QZ(c2,LoadInteger(e5,cp,eG))
call QZ(c2,LoadInteger(e5,cp,eF))
call QZ(c2,LoadInteger(e5,cp,eN))
call QZ(c2,LoadInteger(e5,cp,e2))
endfunction
function Q5 takes integer c2,integer cp returns nothing
call SaveInteger(cq,IA(FT,c2),F5[c2],cp)
set F5[c2]=F5[c2]+1
endfunction
function QT takes integer c2 returns integer
local integer cp=LoadInteger(cq,IA(FT,c2),FD[c2])
set FD[c2]=FD[c2]+1
return cp
endfunction
function QC takes integer c2 returns boolean
return FD[c2]<F5[c2]
endfunction
function Q4 takes nothing returns nothing
local integer c6=ay()
set FB[c6]=FB[c6]-.05
set FA[c6]=FA[c6]+10
if FB[c6]>1. then
if GetLocalPlayer()==co[FU[c6]-1] then
call BlzFrameSetAlpha(Fj[FH[c6]],FA[c6])
endif
if GetLocalPlayer()==co[FU[c6]-1] then
call av(Fj[FH[c6]],.03555,.03555,.35+.04*FH[c6],.14,FB[c6],FB[c6])
endif
endif
if Fs[c6]<16 then
if GetLocalPlayer()==co[FU[c6]-1] then
call BlzFrameSetAlpha(Fj[FH[c6]],$FF-14*(17-Fs[c6]))
endif
if GetLocalPlayer()==co[FU[c6]-1] then
call ac(Fj[FH[c6]],.03555,.03555,.35+.04*FH[c6],.14+.005*(17-Fs[c6]))
endif
endif
set Fs[c6]=Fs[c6]-1
if Fs[c6]<=0 then
set Fi[FU[c6]]=Fi[FU[c6]]-1
if Fi[FU[c6]]==0 then
set Fn[FU[c6]]=0
endif
call dP(FU[c6]-1,Fj[FH[c6]])
if QC(FU[c6]) then
call x4(FU[c6],QT(FU[c6]))
endif
call a0()
call xG(c6)
endif
endfunction
function Qw takes integer c2,integer e7,integer cp returns nothing
local integer c6=xw()
set Fs[c6]=48
set FU[c6]=c2
set FH[c6]=e7
set FB[c6]=2.
call aH(c2-1,Fj[e7],"war3mapImported\\HeroIcon_"+I2S(cp)+".blp")
if GetLocalPlayer()==co[FU[c6]-1] then
call av(Fj[FH[c6]],.03555,.03555,.35+.04*e7,.14,FB[c6],FB[c6])
endif
set FA[c6]=55
if GetLocalPlayer()==co[FU[c6]-1] then
call BlzFrameSetAlpha(Fj[FH[c6]],50)
endif
call dg(FU[c6]-1,Fj[e7])
set Fi[FU[c6]]=Fi[FU[c6]]+1
call aR(c6,iP,true,function Q4)
endfunction
function QF takes integer c2,integer CX returns nothing
local integer iX=0
local unit c4=null
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(CQ,c2),CX)!=0 then
set c4=Bs[c2*5+CX]
if not ds(c4) then
set iX=R2I(GetUnitState(c4,UNIT_STATE_LIFE)/GetUnitState(c4,UNIT_STATE_MAX_LIFE)*$64+.1)
if iX!=LoadInteger(cq,IA(Fe,c2),CX) then
if iX<=25 then
call dl(c2-1,Cp[CX],"|cfff85555"+I2S(iX)+"%|r")
elseif iX<=50 then
call dl(c2-1,Cp[CX],"|cffffff00"+I2S(iX)+"%|r")
elseif iX<=75 then
call dl(c2-1,Cp[CX],"|cffafff54"+I2S(iX)+"%|r")
else
call dl(c2-1,Cp[CX],"|cff7fff00"+I2S(iX)+"%|r")
endif
call SaveInteger(cq,IA(Fe,c2),CX,iX)
endif
else
call dl(c2-1,Cp[CX],"|cfff85555"+I2S(R2I(LoadReal(cq,IA(Cf,c2),CX)))+"s|r")
endif
endif
set c4=null
endfunction
function QN takes nothing returns nothing
local integer c6=ay()
call QF(F1[c6],1)
call QF(F1[c6],2)
call QF(F1[c6],3)
call QF(F1[c6],4)
call QF(F1[c6],5)
if wu[F1[c6]] or cY[F1[c6]]<=0 then
call a0()
call xC(c6)
endif
endfunction
function QY takes boolean YX,code NJ returns nothing
local integer cz
local trigger N9=CreateTrigger()
set cz=0
loop
if YX then
call TriggerRegisterPlayerUnitEvent(N9,Player(cz),EVENT_PLAYER_UNIT_SELECTED,null)
else
call TriggerRegisterPlayerUnitEvent(N9,Player(cz),EVENT_PLAYER_UNIT_DESELECTED,null)
endif
set cz=cz+1
exitwhen cz==GetBJMaxPlayerSlots()
endloop
call TriggerAddAction(N9,NJ)
set N9=null
endfunction
function QP takes nothing returns nothing
local player cC=GetTriggerPlayer()
local integer cV=GetPlayerId(cC)
local unit c4=GetTriggerUnit()
local integer cp=0
if GetTriggerEventId()==EVENT_PLAYER_UNIT_SELECTED then
if GetLocalPlayer()==cC then
call BlzFrameSetText(Ng,GetUnitName(c4))
endif
set Na=LoadInteger(eX,GetHandleId(c4),L0)
if Na>0 and not (GetUnitAbilityLevel(c4,$5A657235)>0) then
set GP="war3mapImported\\HeroIcon_"+I2S(Na)+".blp"
else
if IsUnitType(c4,UNIT_TYPE_TAUREN) then
set GP="war3mapImported\\Icon_Boss.blp"
else
set GP="war3mapImported\\Icon_Creep.blp"
set cp=LoadInteger(i9,GetUnitTypeId(c4),iE)
endif
endif
if GetLocalPlayer()==cC then
call aU(NP,GP)
endif
call dl(cV,Nm[1],iK+": "+I2S(R2I(GetUnitState(c4,UNIT_STATE_LIFE)+.1))+"/"+I2S(R2I(GetUnitState(c4,UNIT_STATE_MAX_LIFE)+.1)))
call dl(cV,Nm[2],iO+": "+I2S(R2I(GetUnitState(c4,UNIT_STATE_MANA)+.1))+"/"+I2S(R2I(GetUnitState(c4,UNIT_STATE_MAX_MANA)+.1)))
call dl(cV,Nm[3],": "+I2S(pz(c4)))
call dl(cV,Nm[4],": "+I2S(R2I(LoadReal(eX,GetHandleId(c4),eq)*$64+.1))+"%")
call dl(cV,Nm[5],wF+": "+I2S(pr(c4)))
call dl(cV,Nm[6],wF+": "+I2S(R2I(LoadReal(eX,GetHandleId(c4),e6)*$64+.1))+"%")
if cp>=1 and cp<=60 then
call dl(cV,No,BlzGetAbilityTooltip($41303251,cp-1))
else
call dl(cV,No,"")
endif
set Yq[cV]=c4
endif
set cC=null
set c4=null
endfunction
function Qg takes nothing returns nothing
call QY(true,function QP)
call QY(false,function QP)
endfunction
function Qm takes integer c2,integer cp returns boolean
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(CQ,c2),1)==cp then
return false
endif
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(CQ,c2),2)==cp then
return false
endif
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(CQ,c2),3)==cp then
return false
endif
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(CQ,c2),4)==cp then
return false
endif
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(CQ,c2),5)==cp then
return false
endif
return true
endfunction
function QV takes unit c4 returns nothing
local integer cV=GetUnitTypeId(c4)
local real i3=LoadReal(i9,cV,eq)
if not ds(c4) then
if i3>0 then
call fL(c4,eq,i3)
endif
set i3=LoadReal(i9,cV,e6)
if i3>0 then
call fL(c4,e6,i3)
endif
set i3=LoadReal(i9,cV,ez)
if i3>0 then
call fL(c4,ez,i3)
endif
set i3=LoadReal(i9,cV,eR)
if i3>0 then
call fL(c4,eR,i3)
endif
set i3=LoadReal(i9,cV,ih)
if i3>0 then
call fL(c4,ih,i3)
endif
set i3=LoadReal(i9,cV,er)
if i3>0 then
call fL(c4,er,i3)
endif
set i3=LoadReal(i9,cV,iZ)
if i3>0 then
call fL(c4,iZ,i3)
endif
set i3=LoadReal(i9,cV,eK)
if i3>0 then
call fL(c4,eK,i3)
endif
set i3=LoadReal(i9,cV,eO)
if i3>0 then
call fL(c4,eO,i3)
endif
endif
endfunction
function Qx takes unit c4,integer c2 returns nothing
local integer cV=GetUnitTypeId(c4)
call SaveInteger(eX,GetHandleId(c4),Ds,LoadInteger(i9,cV,Ds))
call SaveInteger(eX,GetHandleId(c4),iA,LoadInteger(i9,cV,iA))
call SaveInteger(eX,GetHandleId(c4),ey,LoadInteger(i9,cV,ey))
call SaveInteger(eX,GetHandleId(c4),L0,LoadInteger(i9,cV,L0))
call QV(c4)
call xB(c4)
call V5(c4,c2)
endfunction
function Ql takes integer c2,integer CX,integer cp,real Cq,real C6,boolean Cz returns unit
local real Ws=GetRectCenterX(ie[c2])*1.+$384*1.*Cos($B4*1.*bj_DEGTORAD)
local real WU=GetRectCenterY(ie[c2])*1.+$384*1.*Sin($B4*1.*bj_DEGTORAD)
local real ij=Ws*1.+$12C*CX*1.*Cos(0*1.*bj_DEGTORAD)
local real iJ=WU*1.+$12C*CX*1.*Sin(0*1.*bj_DEGTORAD)
local unit c4
if Cq!=0 and C6!=0 then
set ij=Cq
set iJ=C6
endif
set c4=CreateUnit(co[c2-1],DE[cp],ij,iJ,$10E)
call Qx(c4,c2)
call dl(c2-1,Cp[CX],"|cff7fff00100%|r")
call SaveInteger(ef,GetHandleId(c4),ed,c2)
call SaveInteger(eX,GetHandleId(c4),Cx,CX)
call V0(c4,1,LoadInteger(cq,IA(c0,c2),1))
call V0(c4,2,LoadInteger(cq,IA(c0,c2),2))
call V0(c4,3,LoadInteger(cq,IA(c0,c2),3))
call V0(c4,4,LoadInteger(cq,IA(c0,c2),4))
call V0(c4,5,LoadInteger(cq,IA(c0,c2),5))
call V0(c4,6,LoadInteger(cq,IA(c0,c2),6))
call V0(c4,7,LoadInteger(cq,IA(c0,c2),7))
call V0(c4,8,LoadInteger(cq,IA(c0,c2),8))
if not Cz then
call SaveInteger(cq,IA(CO,c2),cp,1)
call x7(c2,cp,1)
call aH(c2-1,Ca[CX],"war3mapImported\\HeroIcon_"+I2S(cp)+".blp")
call dl(c2-1,C0[CX],GetObjectName(DE[cp])+"\n"+C8[LoadInteger(i9,DE[cp],ey)]+BlzGetAbilityActivatedExtendedTooltip($41303237,cp-1))
call aH(c2-1,wc[CX],"war3mapImported\\FrameUI_herobackground"+I2S(LoadInteger(i9,DE[cp],Ds))+".blp")
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(w7,c2),CX)!=LoadInteger(i9,DE[cp],iA) then
call SaveInteger(cq,IA(w7,c2),CX,LoadInteger(i9,DE[cp],iA))
call SaveStr(cq,IA(w1,c2),CX,"war3mapImported\\TB_"+I2S(LoadInteger(cq,IA(w7,c2),CX)+1)+"_1_"+I2S(LoadInteger(cq,IA(w1,c2),CX))+".blp")
call aH(c2-1,we[CX],LoadStr(cq,IA(w1,c2),CX))
call SaveStr(cq,IA(wi,c2),CX,"war3mapImported\\TB_"+I2S(LoadInteger(cq,IA(w7,c2),CX)+1)+"_2_"+I2S(LoadInteger(cq,IA(wi,c2),CX))+".blp")
call aH(c2-1,wn[CX],LoadStr(cq,IA(wi,c2),CX))
call SaveStr(cq,IA(wL,c2),CX,"war3mapImported\\TB_"+I2S(LoadInteger(cq,IA(w7,c2),CX)+1)+"_3_"+I2S(LoadInteger(cq,IA(wL,c2),CX))+".blp")
call aH(c2-1,wW[CX],LoadStr(cq,IA(wL,c2),CX))
endif
endif
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(w1,c2),CX)>0 then
call xL(c4,1,LoadInteger(cq,IA(w1,c2),CX),1)
endif
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(wi,c2),CX)>0 then
call xL(c4,2,LoadInteger(cq,IA(wi,c2),CX),1)
endif
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(wL,c2),CX)>0 then
call xL(c4,3,LoadInteger(cq,IA(wL,c2),CX),1)
endif
call SetUnitState(c4,UNIT_STATE_LIFE,GetUnitState(c4,UNIT_STATE_MAX_LIFE))
call SetUnitState(c4,UNIT_STATE_MANA,GetUnitState(c4,UNIT_STATE_MAX_MANA))
set Bv=c4
set c4=null
return Bv
endfunction
function Qu takes nothing returns boolean
local integer c6=ay()
if not ds(Dj[c6]) then
call SetTextTagPos(DJ[c6],GetUnitX(Dj[c6])-Dh[c6],GetUnitY(Dj[c6]),DZ[c6])
else
call DestroyTextTag(DJ[c6])
set D9[c6]=null
set DJ[c6]=null
set Dj[c6]=null
call a0()
call xJ(c6)
endif
return false
endfunction
function Qb takes unit c4 returns nothing
local integer c6=xj()
local integer cV=GetUnitTypeId(c4)
set Dj[c6]=c4
set DJ[c6]=CreateTextTag()
set D9[c6]=D3[LoadInteger(eX,GetHandleId(c4),Ds)]+" "+GetUnitName(c4)
set Dh[c6]=20+StringLength(D9[c6])*2
set DZ[c6]=$172
if cV==DE[22] or cV==DE[14] or cV==DE[17] or cV==DE[70] then
set DZ[c6]=DZ[c6]+$64
elseif cV==DE[32] or cV==DE[40] or cV==DE[55] or cV==DE[56] or cV==DE[49] then
set DZ[c6]=DZ[c6]+50
endif
call SetTextTagText(DJ[c6],D9[c6],.0207)
call SetTextTagPos(DJ[c6],GetUnitX(Dj[c6])-Dh[c6],GetUnitY(Dj[c6]),DZ[c6])
call SetTextTagPermanent(DJ[c6],true)
call SetTextTagVisibility(DJ[c6],true)
call SetTextTagLifespan(DJ[c6],0)
call aR(c6,iP,true,function Qu)
endfunction
function Qk takes unit c4,integer cp,integer CK,integer i3 returns nothing
if not ds(c4) then
if cp==1 then
call po(c4,CK*$64*i3)
call pV(c4,CK*$64*i3)
call fL(c4,eO,CK*3*i3)
if CK==12 then
call fL(c4,B9,.05)
endif
elseif cp==2 then
call pl(c4,CK*$3E8*i3)
call pu(c4,CK*25*i3)
if CK==1 then
call pI(c4,$3E8)
call pf(c4,25)
endif
if CK==12 then
call fL(c4,vA,.05)
endif
else
call pl(c4,CK*$3E8*i3)
call fL(c4,eq,CK*.01*i3)
call fL(c4,e6,CK*.01*i3)
if CK==1 then
call pI(c4,$3E8)
endif
if CK==12 then
call fL(c4,vA,.05)
endif
endif
endif
endfunction

endfunction

endfunction

function QR takes nothing returns nothing
local framehandle cx=BlzGetTriggerFrame()

endif

else

endif
call x1(cV+1,G5[cV+1])

set cC=null
endfunction
function QO takes nothing returns nothing

local player cC=GetTriggerPlayer()

else
set G5[cV+1]=G5[cV+1]-1
endif
call x1(cV+1,G5[cV+1])

set cC=null
endfunction

call QK(2,.44833)

call Qr(2,.056,.49332)

function QM takes nothing returns nothing
local integer c6=ay()
local integer cp=1
local integer iz=0
local integer wO=0
local boolean cP=false
set GN[c6]=GN[c6]+1
set iz=-5+5*GN[c6]
loop
exitwhen cp>5
set wO=LoadInteger(cq,IA(G2,GG[c6]+GN[c6]),cp)
if wO>0 then
call dg(GF[c6]-1,GY[iz+cp])
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(CO,GF[c6]),wO)>0 then
call aH(GF[c6]-1,GY[iz+cp],"war3mapImported\\HeroIcon_"+I2S(wO)+".blp")
else
call aH(GF[c6]-1,GY[iz+cp],"war3mapImported\\DisHeroIcon_"+I2S(wO)+".blp")
endif
call SaveStr(cq,IA(CO,GF[c6]),iz+cp,GetObjectName(DE[LoadInteger(cq,IA(G2,GG[c6]+GN[c6]),cp)]))
set cP=true
else
call dP(GF[c6]-1,GY[iz+cp])
endif
set cp=cp+1
endloop
if cP then
set GP=BlzGetAbilityTooltip($41303237,GG[c6]+GN[c6]-1)+"\n"
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(WE,GF[c6]),GG[c6]+GN[c6])>=2 then
set GP=GP+"|cffffff00"+BlzGetAbilityExtendedTooltip($41303237,GG[c6]+GN[c6]-1)+"|r"
else
set GP=GP+BlzGetAbilityExtendedTooltip($41303237,GG[c6]+GN[c6]-1)
endif
call dl(GF[c6]-1,Gg[GN[c6]],GP)
else
call dl(GF[c6]-1,Gg[GN[c6]],"")
endif
if GN[c6]>=7 then
call a0()
call xn(c6)
endif
endfunction

exitwhen GO>18

function Xj takes integer c2 returns integer
local integer ec=GetRandomInt(1,8)
local integer Gr=ec
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(ee,c2),ec)<=8 then
return ec
endif
set ec=GetRandomInt(1,8)
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(ee,c2),ec)<=8 then
return ec
endif
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(ee,c2),ec)<LoadInteger(cq,IA(ee,c2),Gr) then
set Gr=ec
endif
set ec=GetRandomInt(1,8)
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(ee,c2),ec)<=8 then
return ec
endif
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(ee,c2),ec)<LoadInteger(cq,IA(ee,c2),Gr) then
set Gr=ec
endif
return Gr
endfunction
function XJ takes nothing returns nothing
local framehandle cx=BlzGetTriggerFrame()
local player cC=GetTriggerPlayer()
local integer c2=GetPlayerId(cC)+1
local integer GO=LoadInteger(es,YO,GetHandleId(cx))
if GetLocalPlayer()==cC then
call BlzFrameSetEnable(cx,false)
call BlzFrameSetEnable(cx,true)
endif
if GO==1 and cg[c2]-cb>=1 then
call Qj(c2,-1)
call QD(c2,Xj(c2),1,true)
call XB(c2,cg[c2]-cb)
elseif GO==2 and cg[c2]-cb>=3 then
call Qj(c2,-3)
call QD(c2,QE(),1,true)
call XB(c2,cg[c2]-cb)
elseif GO==3 then
if not Nr[c2] then
set Nr[c2]=true
set GO=9
loop
exitwhen GO>18
call dg(c2-1,ck[GO])
set GO=GO+1
endloop
call dg(c2-1,ck[57])
call dg(c2-1,ck[58])
else
set Nr[c2]=false
set GO=9
loop
exitwhen GO>18
call dP(c2-1,ck[GO])
set GO=GO+1
endloop
call dP(c2-1,ck[57])
call dP(c2-1,ck[58])
endif
elseif GO>=9 and GO<=18 then
if cg[c2]-cb>=5 then
call Qj(c2,-5)
call QD(c2,GO,1,true)
call XB(c2,cg[c2]-cb)
endif
elseif GO==57 or GO==58 then
if cg[c2]-cb>=5 then
call Qj(c2,-5)
call QD(c2,GO,1,true)
call XB(c2,cg[c2]-cb)
endif
endif
set cx=null
set cC=null
endfunction

function Xp takes nothing returns nothing
local unit cP=GetTriggerUnit()
local unit c4=GetKillingUnit()
local integer c2=dv(c4)
local integer Y8=dv(cP)
local integer cV=GetUnitTypeId(cP)
local integer in=0
if Y8==25 and not (GetUnitAbilityLevel(cP,$41303249)>0) then
if GetUnitAbilityLevel(cP,$41303245)>0 then
call d7(wE[c2]+wA[c2]+"|r"+" đã tiêu diệt "+GetUnitName(cP),5)
call Pj(cP,cV)
else
if cV==GB[17] then
call DestroyEffect(AddSpecialEffectTarget("Abilities\\Spells\\Other\\Stampede\\StampedeMissileDeath.mdl",cP,"chest"))
call Pa(GetUnitX(cP),GetUnitY(cP))
endif

call VJ(c4,2.5,$64)
endif
if cV==GB[55] then
call VJ(c4,3.,$64)
endif
if cV==GB[29] then
call pf(c4,-35)
endif
if cV==GB[50] then
call pa(c4,-.85)
endif
if cV==GB[59] then
call Xa(c4)
endif
if GetUnitAbilityLevel(cP,$41303242)>0 then
set in=$7D0
set Pc[c2]=Pc[c2]-1.
call dL(co[c2-1],"|cffecce87Thời gian hồi sinh -1s|r",GetUnitX(cP),GetUnitY(cP),11,true)
if GetUnitAbilityLevel(cP,$4130324C)>0 then
call QD(c2,QA(),1,true)
else
call QD(c2,QE(),1,true)
endif
elseif GetUnitAbilityLevel(cP,$41303241)>0 then
set in=$3E8
if GetUnitAbilityLevel(cP,$4E4E6750)>0 then
call QD(c2,QE(),1,true)
else
call QD(c2,GetRandomInt(1,8),1,true)
endif
else
set in=4+2*LoadInteger(i9,cV,iE)
endif
call Q9(c2,in)
call dL(co[c2-1],ic++I2S(in),GetUnitX(cP),GetUnitY(cP),11,true)
set CZ[c2]=CZ[c2]-1
call dl(c2-1,CE,I2S(CZ[c2])+"/40")
if CZ[c2]==1 then
call V4(c2,0)
endif
if CZ[c2]==0 then
call Xb(c2,cf)
call dP(c2-1,wN)
call dP(c2-1,w2)
call BlzFrameSetVisible(wY[c2],false)
if cf==wg then
if cY[c2]>0 then
call d7(wE[c2]+wA[c2]+"|r"+ic+" đã hoàn thành trò chơi, xin chúc mừng.",30)
if cY[c2]>=40 and wt[c2]<=$B4 and iv>=4 and cN>=9 then
call d7("Xếp hạng trận chiến: |cffecce87[Huyền Thoại *******]|r. Bạn là minh chứng cho một thời đại!",30)
elseif cY[c2]>=40 and wt[c2]<=$B4 and iv>=3 and cN>=8 then
call d7("Xếp hạng trận chiến: |cfff85555[Vô Song ******]|r. Bạn chính là chiến lược gia đại tài!",30)
elseif cY[c2]>=40 and wt[c2]<=$B4 and cN>=7 then
call d7("Xếp hạng trận chiến: |cffffa500[Tuyệt Đỉnh *****].|r Bạn có phong thái nhà lãnh đạo!",30)
elseif cY[c2]>=35 and wt[c2]<=$F0 then
call d7("Xếp hạng trận chiến: |cffffff00[Siêu Phẩm ****].|r Bạn là kẻ thống trị cuộc chơi!",30)
elseif cY[c2]>=30 and wt[c2]<=$12C then
call d7("Xếp hạng trận chiến: |cffdb70db[Xuất Sắc ***].|r Bạn đã tạo ra kỳ tích!",30)
elseif cY[c2]>=20 and wt[c2]<=$168 then
call d7("Xếp hạng trận chiến: |cff0fdfff[Khá Tốt **].|r Một kết quả ổn định và đáng tự hào!",30)
else
call d7("Xếp hạng trận chiến: |cff7fff00[Sống Sót *].|r Kẻ sống sót là người thắng cuộc!",30)
endif
call VF(ie[c2])
endif
else
if cY[c2]>0 then
call dg(c2-1,wD)
call dl(c2-1,w5,"(Bấm bỏ qua thời gian chờ.)")
endif
call dc(co[c2-1],iK+"Ma Triều kết thúc, hiện tại có thể thao tác với nhân vật xuất chiến.",5)
call Xd(c2,1)
call Xd(c2,2)
call Xd(c2,3)
call Xd(c2,4)
call Xd(c2,5)

function Xq takes nothing returns nothing
local integer c6=ay()
local boolean iz=false
call SaveReal(cq,IA(Cf,Cu[c6]),CV[c6],(LoadReal(cq,IA(Cf,Cu[c6]),CV[c6])-iP)*1.)
if not ds(Bs[Cu[c6]*5+CV[c6]]) or LoadInteger(cq,IA(CQ,Cu[c6]),CV[c6])!=Cl[c6] then
set iz=true
set CI[c6]=0
endif
set CI[c6]=CI[c6]-1
if CI[c6]<=0 then
call SaveBoolean(cq,IA(Cd,Cu[c6]),CV[c6],false)
if not iz then
call aH(Cu[c6]-1,Ca[CV[c6]],"war3mapImported\\HeroIcon_"+I2S(Cl[c6])+".blp")
set Bs[Cu[c6]*5+CV[c6]]=Ql(Cu[c6],CV[c6],Cl[c6],Cb[c6],Ck[c6],true)
call DestroyEffect(AddSpecialEffect("Abilities\\Spells\\Human\\Resurrect\\ResurrectTarget.mdl",GetUnitX(Bs[Cu[c6]*5+CV[c6]])*1.,GetUnitY(Bs[Cu[c6]*5+CV[c6]])*1.))
call VY(Bs[Cu[c6]*5+CV[c6]])
if LoadReal(cq,IA(ws,Cu[c6]),CV[c6])!=0 and LoadReal(cq,IA(wU,Cu[c6]),CV[c6])!=0 then
call IssuePointOrder(Bs[Cu[c6]*5+CV[c6]],"attack",LoadReal(cq,IA(ws,Cu[c6]),CV[c6]),LoadReal(cq,IA(wU,Cu[c6]),CV[c6]))
endif
endif
call a0()
call Vo(c6)
endif
endfunction
function X6 takes unit c4,integer c2,real nX,real ij,real iJ returns nothing

endfunction

function qW takes nothing returns nothing
local integer c6=ay()

set n8=n8-1

call SetUnitY(Lv[c6],LB)
call BlzUnitHideAbility(Lv[c6],LU[c6],true)
set Lv[c6]=null
call a0()
call VD(c6)
endif
endfunction
function qv takes real ij,real iJ,integer L1,integer Le returns unit
local integer c6=VA()
if n8>=50 then
call a0()
call VD(c6)
return null
endif
set LW[c6]=10
set n8=n8+1
set Lv[c6]=Ls[n8]
set LU[c6]=L1
call SetUnitX(Lv[c6],ij)
call SetUnitY(Lv[c6],iJ)
call BlzUnitHideAbility(Lv[c6],LU[c6],false)
call SetUnitAbilityLevel(Lv[c6],LU[c6],Le)
call aR(c6,iP,true,function qW)
return Lv[c6]
endfunction
function qU takes unit c4 returns boolean
return GetRandomReal(0,$64)<LoadReal(eX,GetHandleId(c4),Lc) or GetUnitAbilityLevel(c4,$41303244)>0 or GetUnitAbilityLevel(c4,$4230304F)>0
endfunction
function qH takes unit c4 returns real
local real i3=LoadReal(eX,GetHandleId(c4),iD)
if GetUnitAbilityLevel(c4,$42303049)>0 then
set i3=i3+.4
endif
if GetUnitAbilityLevel(c4,$4230305A)>0 then
set i3=i3+.8
endif
return i3
endfunction
function qB takes unit c4 returns boolean
if GetUnitAbilityLevel(c4,$41303238)>0 then
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(WE,dv(c4)),10)>=2 then
return true
endif
endif
if GetUnitAbilityLevel(c4,$41303337)>0 then
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(WE,dv(c4)),22)>=2 then
return true
endif
endif
return false
endfunction
function qj takes unit c4,real n1,real ne returns nothing

return
endif

set ni=1
elseif ni>$64 then
set ni=$64
endif
if GetRandomReal(0,100.)<ne and not ds(c4) then
set L7=qv(ij,iJ,$52357167,R2I(ni))
call IssueTargetOrder(L7,"thunderbolt",c4)
endif
endfunction

elseif ni>$64 then

endfunction

function qZ takes unit c4,real n1,real ne returns nothing

function qE takes nothing returns nothing
if IsItemVisible(GetEnumItem()) then
set s5[sT]=GetEnumItem()
call SetItemVisible(s5[sT],false)
set sT=sT+1
endif
endfunction
function qA takes real LV,real Lx returns boolean
local real s4=0
local real sw=0
call MoveRectTo(sD,LV,Lx)
call EnumItemsInRect(sD,null,function qE)
call SetItemPosition(sC,LV,Lx)
set s4=GetItemX(sC)
set sw=GetItemY(sC)
call SetItemVisible(sC,false)
loop
exitwhen sT<=0
set sT=sT-1
call SetItemVisible(s5[sT],true)
set s5[sT]=null
endloop
return (s4-LV)*(s4-LV)+(sw-Lx)*(sw-Lx)<=sG*sG and not IsTerrainPathable(LV,Lx,PATHING_TYPE_WALKABILITY)
endfunction
function qD takes nothing returns nothing
local integer c6=ay()
set sZ[c6]=sZ[c6]-1
if not ds(s9[c6]) then
set s3[c6]=s3[c6]*1.+sE[c6]*1.*Cos(sA[c6]*1.*bj_DEGTORAD)
set sh[c6]=sh[c6]*1.+sE[c6]*1.*Sin(sA[c6]*1.*bj_DEGTORAD)
if qA(s3[c6],sh[c6]) then
call SetUnitX(s9[c6],s3[c6])
call SetUnitY(s9[c6],sh[c6])
else
set sZ[c6]=0
endif
else
set sZ[c6]=0
endif
if sZ[c6]<=0 then
call IssueImmediateOrder(s9[c6],"stop")
call SaveBoolean(ef,GetHandleId(s9[c6]),ss,false)
call SetUnitPathing(s9[c6],true)
set s9[c6]=null
call a0()
call Vn(c6)
endif
endfunction
function q5 takes unit c4,real sU,real n1,real sH returns nothing
local integer c6=Vi()
set s9[c6]=c4
set s3[c6]=GetUnitX(c4)
set sh[c6]=GetUnitY(c4)
set sZ[c6]=R2I(n1/iP)
set sE[c6]=sU/sZ[c6]
set sA[c6]=sH
call SetUnitPathing(s9[c6],false)
call SaveBoolean(ef,GetHandleId(c4),ss,true)
call aR(c6,iP,true,function qD)
endfunction

function q2 takes nothing returns nothing

call a0()
call oM(c6)
endif
endfunction

function z1 takes unit c4 returns nothing

set Dg[c6]=c4
set Dt[c6]=dv(c4)
set Dm[c6]=I2R(BlzGetUnitBaseDamage(c4,0))*1.5
call aR(c6,.01,true,function z7)
endfunction
function ze takes nothing returns nothing
local integer c6=ay()
local unit e7=null
call GroupEnumUnitsInRange(ia,sI[c6],sf[c6],$15E,ip)
loop
set e7=FirstOfGroup(ia)
exitwhen e7==null
if IsUnitEnemy(e7,sk[c6]) and not ds(e7) then
call Q1(sb[c6],e7,nA,sa[c6])
endif
call GroupRemoveUnit(ia,e7)
endloop
set sd[c6]=sd[c6]-1
if sd[c6]<=0 then
set sb[c6]=null
set sk[c6]=null
call DestroyEffect(sp[c6])
set sp[c6]=null
call a0()
call oA(c6)
endif
endfunction
function zi takes unit c4,unit cP returns nothing
local integer c6=oE()
call SetUnitAnimation(c4,"spell")
set sb[c6]=c4
set sk[c6]=co[dv(c4)-1]
set sI[c6]=GetUnitX(cP)
set sf[c6]=GetUnitY(cP)
set sd[c6]=4
set sa[c6]=I2R(BlzGetUnitBaseDamage(c4,0))*1.
set sp[c6]=fj("war3mapImported\\Effect_hainhan.mdl",sI[c6],sf[c6],$10E,aN(sI[c6],sf[c6])+10)
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(WE,dv(c4)),23)>=2 then
set sa[c6]=sa[c6]*1.1
endif
call aR(c6,1.,true,function ze)
endfunction
function zn takes unit c4,unit cP returns nothing
if not q7(c4) then
if pk(c4,$64) then
call qc(c4,0,8)
call zi(c4,cP)
endif
endif
endfunction
function zL takes nothing returns nothing
local integer c6=ay()
set Dw[c6]=Dw[c6]-1
if Dw[c6]<=0 then

endfunction
function zW takes unit c4,unit cP returns nothing
local integer c6=o3()
local real Ws=GetUnitX(c4)
local real WU=GetUnitY(c4)
local real ij=GetUnitX(cP)
local real iJ=GetUnitY(cP)
local player cC=co[dv(c4)-1]
local real cm=I2R(BlzGetUnitBaseDamage(c4,0))*1.
call q1(c4,cC,ij,iJ,cm,$64,Bb)
call SetUnitAnimation(c4,"spell")
set Dw[c6]=1
set DG[c6]=fj("war3mapImported\\Effect_nhuycon.mdl",Ws,WU,a2(Ws,WU,ij,iJ),aN(Ws,WU)+5)
call BlzSetSpecialEffectScale(DG[c6],.4*1.)
call aR(c6,.5,true,function zL)
endfunction
function zv takes unit c4,unit cP,integer cp returns nothing
local integer c2=dv(c4)
local real iz=0
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(WE,c2),4)>=2 or LoadInteger(cq,IA(WE,c2),5)>=2 then
set iz=10+5*cp
endif
if at(10+10*cp+iz) then
call zW(c4,cP)
endif
if cp>0 then
if not q7(c4) then
if pk(c4,$64) then
call qc(c4,0,25)
if cp==1 then
set iq=GetUnitFacing(c4)
call oB(c4,$68303338,GetUnitX(c4)*1.+50*1.*Cos((iq+90)*1.*bj_DEGTORAD),GetUnitY(c4)*1.+50*1.*Sin((iq+90)*1.*bj_DEGTORAD),iq,c4)
else
call oB(c4,$68303339,GetUnitX(c4)*1.+50*1.*Cos((iq+90)*1.*bj_DEGTORAD),GetUnitY(c4)*1.+50*1.*Sin((iq+90)*1.*bj_DEGTORAD),iq,c4)
call oB(c4,$68303339,GetUnitX(c4)*1.+50*1.*Cos((iq-90)*1.*bj_DEGTORAD),GetUnitY(c4)*1.+50*1.*Sin((iq-90)*1.*bj_DEGTORAD),iq,c4)
endif
endif
endif
endif
endfunction
function zs takes nothing returns nothing
local integer c6=ay()
set DA[c6]=DA[c6]-1
if DA[c6]<=0 or ds(Dv[c6]) then
call DestroyEffect(AddSpecialEffect("war3mapImported\\Effect_Illusion.mdl",GetUnitX(Dv[c6])*1.,GetUnitY(Dv[c6])*1.))
call pm(Dv[c6])
set Dv[c6]=null
call a0()
call oJ(c6)
endif
endfunction
function zU takes unit c4,integer De,real ij,real iJ,real ii,unit Di returns nothing
local integer c6=oj()
local integer c2=dv(c4)
local integer cV=GetUnitTypeId(c4)
set Dv[c6]=CreateUnit(co[c2-1],De,ij,iJ,ii)
call DestroyEffect(AddSpecialEffect("war3mapImported\\Effect_Illusion.mdl",GetUnitX(Dv[c6])*1.,GetUnitY(Dv[c6])*1.))
call SaveInteger(eX,GetHandleId(Dv[c6]),Ds,LoadInteger(i9,cV,Ds))
call SaveInteger(eX,GetHandleId(Dv[c6]),iA,LoadInteger(i9,cV,iA))
call SaveInteger(eX,GetHandleId(Dv[c6]),ey,LoadInteger(i9,cV,ey))
call SaveInteger(eX,GetHandleId(Dv[c6]),L0,LoadInteger(i9,cV,L0))
call SetUnitVertexColor(Dv[c6],$FF,$FF,$FF,$9B)
call fL(Dv[c6],eq,.35)
call fL(Dv[c6],e6,.35)
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(WE,c2),22)>=2 then
call SaveReal(eX,GetHandleId(Dv[c6]),eq,LoadReal(eX,GetHandleId(Di),eq)*1.)
call SaveReal(eX,GetHandleId(Dv[c6]),e6,LoadReal(eX,GetHandleId(Di),e6)*1.)
call BlzSetUnitBaseDamage(Dv[c6],BlzGetUnitBaseDamage(Di,0),0)
call BlzSetUnitMaxHP(Dv[c6],BlzGetUnitMaxHP(Di))
call SaveReal(eX,GetHandleId(Dv[c6]),eR,LoadReal(eX,GetHandleId(Di),eR)*1.)
call SaveReal(eX,GetHandleId(Dv[c6]),eO,LoadReal(eX,GetHandleId(Di),eO)*1.)
call SaveReal(eX,GetHandleId(Dv[c6]),ez,LoadReal(eX,GetHandleId(Di),ez)*1.)
call SaveReal(eX,GetHandleId(Dv[c6]),er,LoadReal(eX,GetHandleId(Di),er)*1.)
call SaveReal(eX,GetHandleId(Dv[c6]),eK,LoadReal(eX,GetHandleId(Di),eK)*1.)
call SaveReal(eX,GetHandleId(Dv[c6]),ih,LoadReal(eX,GetHandleId(Di),ih)*1.)
call SaveReal(eX,GetHandleId(Dv[c6]),iZ,LoadReal(eX,GetHandleId(Di),iZ)*1.)
call SaveReal(eX,GetHandleId(Dv[c6]),B9,LoadReal(eX,GetHandleId(Di),B9)*1.)
call SaveReal(eX,GetHandleId(Dv[c6]),vA,LoadReal(eX,GetHandleId(Di),vA)*1.)
endif
call Qb(Dv[c6])
call SaveInteger(ef,GetHandleId(Dv[c6]),ed,c2)
call SetUnitState(Dv[c6],UNIT_STATE_LIFE,GetUnitState(Dv[c6],UNIT_STATE_MAX_LIFE))
call SetUnitState(Dv[c6],UNIT_STATE_MANA,GetUnitState(Dv[c6],UNIT_STATE_MAX_MANA))
set DA[c6]=20
call aR(c6,1.,true,function zs)
endfunction
function zH takes nothing returns nothing
local integer c6=ay()
local unit e7=null
local real i3=0
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(WE,UW[c6]),4)>=2 or LoadInteger(cq,IA(WE,UW[c6]),5)>=2 then
set i3=.05+.05*Uv[c6]
endif
call GroupEnumUnitsInRange(ia,UU[c6],UH[c6],$1A4,ip)
loop
set e7=FirstOfGroup(ia)
exitwhen e7==null
if IsUnitEnemy(e7,Us[c6]) and not ds(e7) then
if UB[c6]==6 and not IsUnitType(e7,UNIT_TYPE_TAUREN) and i3>0 then
call pd(e7,-i3)
endif
call Q1(UL[c6],e7,nA,Uj[c6])
call qJ(e7,2.,$64)
endif
call GroupRemoveUnit(ia,e7)
endloop
set UB[c6]=UB[c6]-1
if UB[c6]<=0 then
set UL[c6]=null
set Us[c6]=null
call BlzSetSpecialEffectAlpha(UJ[c6],0)
call DestroyEffect(UJ[c6])
set UJ[c6]=null
call a0()
call oH(c6)
endif
endfunction
function zB takes unit c4,unit cP,integer cp returns nothing
local integer c6=oU()
call SetUnitAnimation(c4,"spell")
set UL[c6]=c4
set UW[c6]=dv(c4)
set Uv[c6]=cp
set Us[c6]=co[UW[c6]-1]
set UU[c6]=GetUnitX(cP)
set UH[c6]=GetUnitY(cP)
set UB[c6]=6
set Uj[c6]=I2R(BlzGetUnitBaseDamage(c4,0))*(.6+.4*cp)
set UJ[c6]=fj("war3mapImported\\Effect_khancochu.mdl",UU[c6],UH[c6],$10E,aN(UU[c6],UH[c6])+30)
call aR(c6,1.,true,function zH)
endfunction

endif
endfunction
function zJ takes nothing returns nothing
local integer c6=ay()
local unit e7
call GroupEnumUnitsInRange(ia,UD[c6],U5[c6],$5DC,ip)
loop
set e7=FirstOfGroup(ia)
exitwhen e7==null
if IsUnitEnemy(e7,UA[c6]) and not ds(e7) and not IsUnitInGroup(e7,U4[c6]) then
call DestroyEffect(AddSpecialEffect("war3mapImported\\Effect_thienloi.mdl",GetUnitX(e7)*1.,GetUnitY(e7)*1.))
call Q1(UZ[c6],e7,nA,UC[c6])
call GroupAddUnit(U4[c6],e7)
call GroupClear(ia)
endif
call GroupRemoveUnit(ia,e7)
endloop
set UT[c6]=UT[c6]-1
if UT[c6]<=0 then
set UZ[c6]=null
set UA[c6]=null
call DestroyGroup(U4[c6])
set U4[c6]=null
call a0()
call ov(c6)
endif
endfunction
function z9 takes unit c4 returns nothing
local integer c6=oW()

call SetUnitAnimation(c4,"spell")
set UZ[c6]=c4
set UE[c6]=dv(c4)
set UA[c6]=co[UE[c6]-1]

set UT[c6]=7
set UC[c6]=I2R(BlzGetUnitBaseDamage(c4,0))*6.6
set U4[c6]=CreateGroup()
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(WE,UE[c6]),8)>=2 then
set Uw=Uw+.2
set UT[c6]=10
endif
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(WE,UE[c6]),23)>=2 then
set Uw=Uw+.1
endif
set UC[c6]=UC[c6]*Uw
call aR(c6,.3,true,function zJ)
endfunction

function zh takes nothing returns nothing
local integer c6=ay()
local unit e7
set LP[c6]=LP[c6]*1.+LN[c6]*1.*Cos(Lt[c6]*1.*bj_DEGTORAD)
set Lg[c6]=Lg[c6]*1.+LN[c6]*1.*Sin(Lt[c6]*1.*bj_DEGTORAD)
call GroupEnumUnitsInRange(ia,LP[c6],Lg[c6],90,ip)
loop
set e7=FirstOfGroup(ia)
exitwhen e7==null
if IsUnitEnemy(e7,LY[c6]) and not ds(e7) and not IsUnitInGroup(e7,Lb[c6]) then
call Q1(L2[c6],e7,nA,Lu[c6])
call GroupAddUnit(Lb[c6],e7)
endif
call GroupRemoveUnit(ia,e7)
endloop
set Ll[c6]=Ll[c6]-1
if Ll[c6]<=0 then
set L2[c6]=null
set LY[c6]=null
call DestroyGroup(Lb[c6])
set Lb[c6]=null
call DestroyEffect(Lf[c6])
set Lf[c6]=null
call a0()
call on(c6)
endif
endfunction

endfunction

function zg takes unit c4,unit cP returns nothing
local integer c6=mp()
local unit e7
local real ij=GetUnitX(cP)
local real iJ=GetUnitY(cP)
local player cC=co[dv(c4)-1]
local integer LT=0
set AD[c6]=c4
set A5[c6]=R2I(15/iP)
call UnitAddAbility(AD[c6],$577A536A)
call DestroyEffect(AddSpecialEffect("war3mapImported\\Effect_linhlungthap.mdl",ij*1.,iJ*1.))
call SetUnitAnimation(c4,"spell")
call GroupEnumUnitsInRange(ia,ij,iJ,$C8,ip)
loop
set e7=FirstOfGroup(ia)
exitwhen e7==null
if IsUnitEnemy(e7,cC) and not ds(e7) then
call qj(e7,3.,$64)
set LT=LT+1
endif
call GroupRemoveUnit(ia,e7)
endloop
if LT>0 then
call pd(AD[c6],.02*LT)
endif
set cC=null
call aR(c6,iP,true,function zP)
endfunction

function zu takes unit c4,integer c2 returns nothing
local integer c6=mx()
set E0[c6]=c4
set E8[c6]=c2
set Ac[c6]=0
set A7[c6]=R2I(30/iP)
call po(E0[c6],40)
if A1[E8[c6]]<16 and LoadInteger(cq,IA(WE,E8[c6]),3)>=2 then
set A1[E8[c6]]=A1[E8[c6]]+1
set Ac[c6]=.03
call pb(E0[c6],.03)

function zb takes nothing returns nothing

set Ez[c6]=0

else

function rs takes nothing returns nothing
local integer c6=ay()
local unit e7
local real ij=0
local real iJ=0
local unit cP=null
call GroupEnumUnitsInRange(ia,Z1[c6],Ze[c6],$3E8,ip)
loop
set e7=FirstOfGroup(ia)
exitwhen e7==null
if IsUnitEnemy(e7,Z7[c6]) and not ds(e7) and not IsUnitInGroup(e7,ZL[c6]) then
set cP=e7
call GroupAddUnit(ZL[c6],e7)
call GroupClear(ia)
endif
call GroupRemoveUnit(ia,e7)
endloop
if cP!=null then
set ij=GetUnitX(cP)
set iJ=GetUnitY(cP)
call SetUnitX(h8[c6],ij)
call SetUnitY(h8[c6],iJ)
call SetUnitAnimation(h8[c6],"attack")
call DestroyEffect(AddSpecialEffect("Abilities\\Weapons\\PhoenixMissile\\Phoenix_Missile.mdl",ij*1.,iJ*1.))
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(WE,Zc[c6]),9)>=2 then
call pd(h8[c6],.06)
set Zv[c6]=Zv[c6]+5
call fL(h8[c6],eR,5)

call q1(h8[c6],Z7[c6],ij,iJ,Zn[c6],$B4,Bb)
endif
set Zi[c6]=Zi[c6]-1
if Zi[c6]<=0 then
if Zv[c6]>0 then
call fL(h8[c6],eR,-Zv[c6])
endif
call fL(h8[c6],ez,-1000)
call DestroyEffect(ZW[c6])
set ZW[c6]=null
call SetUnitVertexColor(h8[c6],$FF,$FF,$FF,$FF)
call SetUnitTimeScale(h8[c6],1.)
set h8[c6]=null
set Z7[c6]=null
call DestroyGroup(ZL[c6])
set ZL[c6]=null
call a0()
call mc(c6)
endif
endfunction

if aY(hY[c6],hP[c6],GetUnitX(h2[c6]),GetUnitY(h2[c6]))<=60 then
call Q1(hN[c6],h2[c6],Bb,hm[c6])
call DestroyEffect(AddSpecialEffectTarget("Abilities\\Weapons\\LordofFlameMissile\\LordofFlameMissile.mdl",h2[c6],"chest"))
set ht[c6]=0
else
set hg[c6]=a2(hY[c6],hP[c6],GetUnitX(h2[c6]),GetUnitY(h2[c6]))
set hY[c6]=hY[c6]*1.+hF[c6]*1.*Cos(hg[c6]*1.*bj_DEGTORAD)
set hP[c6]=hP[c6]*1.+hF[c6]*1.*Sin(hg[c6]*1.*bj_DEGTORAD)
call BlzSetSpecialEffectYaw(hV[c6],hg[c6]*1.*bj_DEGTORAD)
call BlzSetSpecialEffectPosition(hV[c6],hY[c6],hP[c6],ho[c6])
endif
else
set ht[c6]=0
endif
set ht[c6]=ht[c6]-1
if ht[c6]<=0 then
set hN[c6]=null
set h2[c6]=null
call DestroyEffect(hV[c6])
set hV[c6]=null
call a0()
call tK(c6)
endif
endfunction
function rh takes unit c4,real cm,real ij,real iJ,unit cP returns nothing
local integer c6=tr()
set hN[c6]=c4
set h2[c6]=cP
set hY[c6]=ij
set hP[c6]=iJ
set hg[c6]=a2(hY[c6],hP[c6],GetUnitX(cP),GetUnitY(cP))
set ht[c6]=50
set hm[c6]=cm
set ho[c6]=aN(hY[c6],hP[c6])+$64
set hV[c6]=fj("war3mapImported\\Effect_tendo.mdl",hY[c6],hP[c6],hg[c6],ho[c6])
call aR(c6,iP,true,function r3)
endfunction
function rZ takes unit c4,unit cP,integer cp returns nothing
local unit e7=null
local real ij=GetUnitX(c4)
local real iJ=GetUnitY(c4)
local real cm=I2R(BlzGetUnitBaseDamage(c4,0))
local player cC=co[dv(c4)-1]
local integer LT=4
call rh(c4,cm,ij,iJ,cP)
if cp>1 then
call GroupEnumUnitsInRange(ia,ij,iJ,$320,ip)
loop
set e7=FirstOfGroup(ia)
exitwhen e7==null
if IsUnitEnemy(e7,cC) and not ds(e7) and e7!=cP then
call rh(c4,cm,ij,iJ,e7)
set LT=LT-1
if LT==0 then
call GroupClear(ia)
endif
endif
call GroupRemoveUnit(ia,e7)
endloop
endif
set cC=null
endfunction
function rE takes nothing returns nothing
local integer c6=ay()
local unit e7
local real iX=.12
if hD[c6]==6 and LoadInteger(cq,IA(WE,hE[c6]),6)>=2 then
set iX=.3
endif
call GroupEnumUnitsInRange(ia,hh[c6],hZ[c6],$4B0,ip)
loop
set e7=FirstOfGroup(ia)
exitwhen e7==null
if IsUnitAlly(e7,hA[c6]) and not ds(e7) then
call pd(e7,iX)
call pa(e7,.12)
endif
call GroupRemoveUnit(ia,e7)
endloop
set hD[c6]=hD[c6]-1
if hD[c6]==0 or ds(h3[c6]) then
call DestroyEffect(hT[c6])
set hT[c6]=null
call UnitRemoveAbility(h3[c6],h5)
set h3[c6]=null
set hA[c6]=null
call a0()
call t6(c6)
endif
endfunction
function rA takes unit c4 returns nothing
local integer c6=tq()
set h3[c6]=c4
set hh[c6]=GetUnitX(c4)
set hZ[c6]=GetUnitY(c4)
set hE[c6]=dv(c4)
set hA[c6]=co[hE[c6]-1]
set hD[c6]=6
call dB(h3[c6],h5)
set hT[c6]=fj("war3mapImported\\Effect_sinhsinhbattan.mdx",hh[c6],hZ[c6],$10E,aN(hh[c6],hZ[c6]))
call BlzSetSpecialEffectScale(hT[c6],1.5*1.)
call aR(c6,1.,true,function rE)
endfunction

function r5 takes unit c4,unit cP returns nothing
local unit e7
local real ij=GetUnitX(cP)
local real iJ=GetUnitY(cP)
local real cm=0
local integer c2=dv(c4)
local player cC=co[c2-1]
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(WE,c2),16)>=2 then
set cm=I2R(BlzGetUnitBaseDamage(c4,0))*9.
if not ds(UG[c2]) then
call pd(UG[c2],.4)
call DestroyEffect(AddSpecialEffectTarget("war3mapImported\\Effect_thaicuc.mdl",UG[c2],"origin"))
call DestroyEffect(AddSpecialEffectTarget("war3mapImported\\Effect_thaicuc.mdl",c4,"origin"))
endif
else
set cm=I2R(BlzGetUnitBaseDamage(c4,0))*7.5
endif
call SetUnitAnimation(c4,"spell")
call DestroyEffect(AddSpecialEffectTarget("Abilities\\Weapons\\Bolt\\BoltImpact.mdl",c4,"origin"))
call DestroyEffect(AddSpecialEffect("war3mapImported\\Effect_loidinh.mdl",ij*1.,iJ*1.))
call GroupEnumUnitsInRange(ia,ij,iJ,$12C,ip)
loop
set e7=FirstOfGroup(ia)
exitwhen e7==null
if IsUnitEnemy(e7,cC) and not ds(e7) then
call Q1(c4,e7,nA,cm)
call qj(e7,2.,$64)
endif
call GroupRemoveUnit(ia,e7)
endloop
set cC=null
endfunction

call SaveInteger(eX,GetHandleId(j6[c6]),ey,2)
call fL(j6[c6],eq,.75)
call fL(j6[c6],e6,.75)

call SetUnitState(j6[c6],UNIT_STATE_MANA,GetUnitState(j6[c6],UNIT_STATE_MAX_MANA))

set jr[c6]=45
call P2(j6[c6])

endfunction

function r6 takes nothing returns nothing

set jP[c6]=null

endif
endfunction
function rz takes unit c4,unit cP returns nothing
local integer c6=t3()

set jV[c6]=I2R(BlzGetUnitBaseDamage(c4,0))*10.
set jx[c6]=CreateGroup()
