function Kh takes nothing returns nothing
local integer c6=ay()
local unit e7=null
local integer LT=0
local real i3=0
local real iz=0
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(WE,BC[c6]),13)>=2 then
set iz=$3E8
endif
if GetUnitState(BT[c6],UNIT_STATE_MANA)>=4 then
call GroupEnumUnitsInRange(ia,GetUnitX(BT[c6]),GetUnitY(BT[c6]),$3E8,ip)
loop
set e7=FirstOfGroup(ia)
exitwhen e7==null
if IsUnitEnemy(e7,B4[c6]) and not ds(e7) then
if GetUnitState(e7,UNIT_STATE_LIFE)>20 then
call DestroyEffect(AddSpecialEffect("Environment\\LargeBuildingFire\\LargeBuildingFire1.mdl",GetUnitX(e7)*1.,GetUnitY(e7)*1.))
set i3=$2EE
if not IsUnitType(e7,UNIT_TYPE_TAUREN) then
set i3=i3+GetUnitState(e7,UNIT_STATE_MAX_LIFE)*.03
endif
if iz>0 and GetRandomReal(0,$64)<=15 then
set i3=i3+iz
endif
call pI(e7,-i3)
set LT=LT+1
if LT==8 then
call GroupClear(ia)
endif
endif
endif
call GroupRemoveUnit(ia,e7)
endloop
if LT>0 then
call pf(BT[c6],-4)
endif
endif
if ds(BT[c6]) then
set BT[c6]=null
set B4[c6]=null
call a0()
call gq(c6)
endif
endfunction

set HX[c6]=HX[c6]*1.+Hf[c6]*1.*Sin(Hq[c6]*1.*bj_DEGTORAD)
call BlzSetSpecialEffectPosition(HK[c6],HQ[c6],HX[c6],Hr[c6])
call GroupEnumUnitsInRange(ia,HQ[c6],HX[c6],90,ip)
loop
set e7=FirstOfGroup(ia)
exitwhen e7==null
if IsUnitEnemy(e7,Hp[c6]) and not ds(e7) then
set H6[c6]=0
set HQ[c6]=GetUnitX(e7)
set HX[c6]=GetUnitY(e7)
call GroupClear(ia)
endif
call GroupRemoveUnit(ia,e7)
endloop
set H6[c6]=H6[c6]-1
if H6[c6]<=0 then
call q1(Hd[c6],Hp[c6],HQ[c6],HX[c6],Hz[c6],$96,nA)
set Hd[c6]=null
set Hp[c6]=null
call DestroyEffect(HK[c6])
set HK[c6]=null
call a0()
call gP(c6)
endif
endfunction
function KP takes unit c4,unit cP returns nothing
local integer c6=gY()
set Hd[c6]=c4
set Ha[c6]=dv(c4)
set Hp[c6]=co[Ha[c6]-1]
set HQ[c6]=GetUnitX(c4)
set HX[c6]=GetUnitY(c4)
set Hq[c6]=a2(HQ[c6],HX[c6],GetUnitX(cP),GetUnitY(cP))
set H6[c6]=50
set Hz[c6]=I2R(BlzGetUnitBaseDamage(c4,0))
set Hr[c6]=aN(HQ[c6],HX[c6])+$64
set HK[c6]=fj("war3mapImported\\Effect_quangcau.mdl",HQ[c6],HX[c6],Hq[c6],Hr[c6])
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(WE,Ha[c6]),23)>=2 then
set Hz[c6]=Hz[c6]*1.1
if Uk[Ha[c6]]<10 then
set Uk[Ha[c6]]=Uk[Ha[c6]]+1
endif
endif
call aR(c6,iP,true,function KY)
endfunction
function Kg takes nothing returns nothing
local integer c6=ay()
local unit e7=null
local unit cx=null
local real Hl=0
local real sU=0
local real i3=$320
if not ds(HG[c6]) then
set Hl=aY(H2[c6],HY[c6],GetUnitX(HG[c6]),GetUnitY(HG[c6]))/Hm[c6]
set HP[c6]=a2(H2[c6],HY[c6],GetUnitX(HG[c6]),GetUnitY(HG[c6]))
call BlzSetSpecialEffectYaw(Hx[c6],HP[c6]*1.*bj_DEGTORAD)
set H2[c6]=H2[c6]*1.+Hl*1.*Cos(HP[c6]*1.*bj_DEGTORAD)
set HY[c6]=HY[c6]*1.+Hl*1.*Sin(HP[c6]*1.*bj_DEGTORAD)
call BlzSetSpecialEffectPosition(Hx[c6],H2[c6],HY[c6],Hg[c6])
if Hm[c6]==1 then
set vs=true
call Q1(Hw[c6],HG[c6],nA,Ho[c6])
call DestroyEffect(AddSpecialEffectTarget("Abilities\\Weapons\\FarseerMissile\\FarseerMissile.mdl",HG[c6],"chest"))
set HG[c6]=null
endif
endif
set Hm[c6]=Hm[c6]-1
if Hm[c6]==0 then
set Hm[c6]=10
call GroupEnumUnitsInRange(ia,H2[c6],HY[c6],$320,ip)
loop
set e7=FirstOfGroup(ia)
exitwhen e7==null
if IsUnitEnemy(e7,HN[c6]) and not ds(e7) and not IsUnitInGroup(e7,HV[c6]) then
set sU=aY(H2[c6],HY[c6],GetUnitX(e7),GetUnitY(e7))
if sU<=i3 then
set i3=sU
set cx=e7
endif
endif
call GroupRemoveUnit(ia,e7)
endloop
if cx!=null then
set HG[c6]=cx
call GroupAddUnit(HV[c6],cx)
endif
if HG[c6]==null then
set Ht[c6]=0
endif
endif
set Ht[c6]=Ht[c6]-1
if Ht[c6]<=0 then
set Hw[c6]=null
set HG[c6]=null
set HN[c6]=null
call DestroyGroup(HV[c6])
set HV[c6]=null
call DestroyEffect(Hx[c6])
set Hx[c6]=null
call a0()
call gN(c6)
endif
endfunction

set Hg[c6]=aN(H2[c6],HY[c6])+$6E
set Ht[c6]=70
set Hm[c6]=10

set Ho[c6]=Ho[c6]*1.1
endif

endfunction

function Kl takes nothing returns nothing
local integer c6=ay()
local unit e7
set Um[c6]=Um[c6]*1.+UY[c6]*1.*Cos(UV[c6]*1.*bj_DEGTORAD)
set Uo[c6]=Uo[c6]*1.+UY[c6]*1.*Sin(UV[c6]*1.*bj_DEGTORAD)
call BlzSetSpecialEffectPosition(UI[c6],Um[c6],Uo[c6],Ub[c6])
call GroupEnumUnitsInRange(ia,Um[c6],Uo[c6],$C8,ip)
loop
set e7=FirstOfGroup(ia)
exitwhen e7==null
if IsUnitEnemy(e7,Ut[c6]) and not ds(e7) and not IsUnitInGroup(e7,Uu[c6]) then
call Q1(UP[c6],e7,nA,Ul[c6])
call qJ(e7,3.,$64)
if not IsUnitType(e7,UNIT_TYPE_TAUREN) then
call qC(e7,$78,UV[c6],.2,$64)
endif
call GroupAddUnit(Uu[c6],e7)
endif
call GroupRemoveUnit(ia,e7)
endloop
set Ux[c6]=Ux[c6]-1
if Ux[c6]<=0 then
set UP[c6]=null
set Ut[c6]=null
call DestroyGroup(Uu[c6])
set Uu[c6]=null
call DestroyEffect(UI[c6])
set UI[c6]=null
call a0()
call gT(c6)
endif
endfunction
function Ku takes unit c4,unit cP returns nothing
local integer c6=g5()
call SetUnitAnimation(c4,"spell")
call DestroyEffect(AddSpecialEffectTarget("Abilities\\Weapons\\DragonHawkMissile\\DragonHawkMissile.mdl",c4,"hand right"))
set UP[c6]=c4
set Ug[c6]=dv(c4)
set Ut[c6]=co[Ug[c6]-1]
set Um[c6]=GetUnitX(c4)
set Uo[c6]=GetUnitY(c4)
set UV[c6]=a2(Um[c6],Uo[c6],GetUnitX(cP),GetUnitY(cP))
set Ux[c6]=R2I($384/UY[c6])
set Ul[c6]=I2R(BlzGetUnitBaseDamage(c4,0))*8.
set Uu[c6]=CreateGroup()
set Ub[c6]=aN(Um[c6],Uo[c6])
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(WE,Ug[c6]),23)>=2 then
if Uk[Ug[c6]]>0 then
set UI[c6]=fj("war3mapImported\\Effect_cuongphongloi.mdl",Um[c6],Uo[c6],UV[c6],Ub[c6])
else
set UI[c6]=fj("war3mapImported\\Effect_cuongphongbaotau.mdl",Um[c6],Uo[c6],UV[c6],Ub[c6])
call BlzSetSpecialEffectScale(UI[c6],2.5*1.)
endif
set Ul[c6]=Ul[c6]*(1.1+Uk[Ug[c6]]*.05)
set Uk[Ug[c6]]=0
else
set UI[c6]=fj("war3mapImported\\Effect_cuongphongbaotau.mdl",Um[c6],Uo[c6],UV[c6],Ub[c6])
call BlzSetSpecialEffectScale(UI[c6],2.5*1.)
endif
call aR(c6,iP,true,function Kl)
endfunction
function Kb takes nothing returns nothing
local integer c6=ay()
if vx[c6]==6 then
call BlzPlaySpecialEffectWithTimeScale(vl[c6],ANIM_TYPE_SPELL,2.)
if vV[c6]==1 then
call qV(vm[c6],vo[c6])
endif
if vV[c6]==2 then
call qk(vm[c6],vo[c6])
endif
if vV[c6]==3 then
call KA(vm[c6],vo[c6])
endif
if vV[c6]==4 then
call qM(vm[c6],vo[c6])
endif
if vV[c6]==5 then
call zi(vm[c6],vo[c6])
endif
if vV[c6]==6 then
call KT(vm[c6],vo[c6])
endif
if vV[c6]==7 then
call zB(vm[c6],vo[c6],1)
endif
if vV[c6]==8 then
call z9(vm[c6])
endif
if vV[c6]==9 then
call zB(vm[c6],vo[c6],2)
endif
if vV[c6]==10 then
call r5(vm[c6],vo[c6])
endif
if vV[c6]==11 then
call Ku(vm[c6],vo[c6])
endif
if vV[c6]==12 then
call rR(vm[c6],vo[c6])
endif
if vV[c6]==13 then
call Kj(vm[c6],vo[c6])
endif
if vV[c6]==14 then
call gL(vm[c6],vo[c6],1)
endif
endif
if vx[c6]==2 then
call SetUnitVertexColor(vm[c6],$FF,$FF,$FF,$FF)
call DestroyEffect(AddSpecialEffect("war3mapImported\\Clone.mdl",GetUnitX(vm[c6])*1.,GetUnitY(vm[c6])*1.))
call PauseUnit(vm[c6],false)
call BlzSetSpecialEffectAlpha(vl[c6],0)
call DestroyEffect(vl[c6])
set vl[c6]=null
endif
set vx[c6]=vx[c6]-1
if vx[c6]<=0 then
call fL(vm[c6],vA,-1.)
set vm[c6]=null
set vo[c6]=null
call a0()
call gA(c6)
endif
endfunction
function Kk takes unit c4,unit cP returns nothing

set vx[c6]=6
set vl[c6]=fj(v2[vV[c6]],Ws,WU,a2(GetUnitX(c4),GetUnitY(c4),ij,iJ),aN(Ws,WU))

call PauseUnit(vm[c6],true)
call fL(vm[c6],vA,1.)
call aR(c6,.5,true,function Kb)
endfunction

function Kf takes nothing returns nothing
local integer c6=ay()
set vw[c6]=vw[c6]-1
if vw[c6]<=0 or ds(v4[c6]) then

set v4[c6]=null
call a0()
call gh(c6)
endif

function Kd takes unit c4,integer c2,real ij,real iJ returns nothing
local integer c6=g3()
set v4[c6]=CreateUnit(co[c2-1],$68303432,ij,iJ,GetUnitFacing(c4))
call DestroyEffect(AddSpecialEffect("Abilities\\Spells\\Undead\\AnimateDead\\AnimateDeadTarget.mdl",GetUnitX(v4[c6])*1.,GetUnitY(v4[c6])*1.))
call SaveInteger(eX,GetHandleId(v4[c6]),ey,5)
call fL(v4[c6],e6,.35)
call fL(v4[c6],eq,1.)
call SaveInteger(ef,GetHandleId(v4[c6]),ed,c2)
set vw[c6]=30
call P2(v4[c6])
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(WE,c2),25)>=2 then
call dB(v4[c6],$41303244)
call BlzSetUnitBaseDamage(v4[c6],$708,0)
call BlzSetUnitMaxHP(v4[c6],$8AAC)
endif
call SetUnitState(v4[c6],UNIT_STATE_LIFE,GetUnitState(v4[c6],UNIT_STATE_MAX_LIFE))
call SetUnitState(v4[c6],UNIT_STATE_MANA,GetUnitState(v4[c6],UNIT_STATE_MAX_MANA))
call aR(c6,1.,true,function Kf)
endfunction
function Ka takes unit c4 returns nothing
local unit e7=null
local integer c2=dv(c4)
local player cC=co[c2-1]
local integer LT=3
local integer cp=1
call SetUnitAnimation(c4,"spell")
call DestroyEffect(AddSpecialEffectTarget("Abilities\\Spells\\Undead\\AnimateDead\\AnimateDeadTarget.mdl",c4,"origin"))
call GroupEnumUnitsInRange(ia,GetUnitX(c4),GetUnitY(c4),$320,ip)
loop
set e7=FirstOfGroup(ia)
exitwhen e7==null
if IsUnitEnemy(e7,cC) and not ds(e7) and not IsUnitType(e7,UNIT_TYPE_TAUREN) then
call DestroyEffect(AddSpecialEffectTarget("war3mapImported\\Effect_bathucot.mdl",e7,"chest"))
call Q1(c4,e7,nA,$F423F)
call Kd(c4,c2,GetUnitX(e7),GetUnitY(e7))
set LT=LT-1
if LT==0 then
call GroupClear(ia)
endif
endif
call GroupRemoveUnit(ia,e7)
endloop
if LT>0 then
loop
exitwhen cp>LT
call Kd(c4,c2,am(GetUnitX(c4),$C8),am(GetUnitY(c4),$C8))
set cp=cp+1
endloop
endif
set cC=null
set e7=null
endfunction

function K6 takes nothing returns nothing
local integer c6=ay()
if not ds(WR[c6]) then
if aY(WO[c6],Wy[c6],GetUnitX(WR[c6]),GetUnitY(WR[c6]))<=60 then
set WO[c6]=GetUnitX(WR[c6])
set Wy[c6]=GetUnitY(WR[c6])
set W0[c6]=0
else
set WM[c6]=a2(WO[c6],Wy[c6],GetUnitX(WR[c6]),GetUnitY(WR[c6]))
set WO[c6]=WO[c6]*1.+Wr[c6]*1.*Cos(WM[c6]*1.*bj_DEGTORAD)
set Wy[c6]=Wy[c6]*1.+Wr[c6]*1.*Sin(WM[c6]*1.*bj_DEGTORAD)
call BlzSetSpecialEffectYaw(vc[c6],WM[c6]*1.*bj_DEGTORAD)
call BlzSetSpecialEffectPosition(vc[c6],WO[c6],Wy[c6],W8[c6])
endif
else
set W0[c6]=0
endif
set W0[c6]=W0[c6]-1
if W0[c6]<=0 then
if v7[c6]==1 then
call gv(WK[c6],WO[c6],Wy[c6])
else
call gs(WK[c6],WO[c6],Wy[c6])
endif
set WK[c6]=null
set WR[c6]=null
call DestroyEffect(vc[c6])
set vc[c6]=null
call a0()
call gH(c6)
endif
endfunction

function Kr takes unit c4,unit cP returns nothing
local integer c2=dv(c4)

set iz=true

endif

call Kz(c4,cP,1)
endif
endif
endfunction
function KK takes unit c4,real ij,real iJ returns nothing
local integer c2=dv(c4)
local real cm=I2R(BlzGetUnitBaseDamage(c4,0))*12.*(1.+vW[c2])
local player cC=co[c2-1]
local unit e7=null
local real vv=$B4
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(WE,c2),24)>=2 then
set vv=$F0
endif
set vW[c2]=0
call UnitRemoveAbility(c4,$41303333)
call fL(c4,eK,$3E8)
call GroupEnumUnitsInRange(ia,ij,iJ,vv,ip)
loop
set e7=FirstOfGroup(ia)
exitwhen e7==null
if IsUnitEnemy(e7,cC) and not ds(e7) then
call DestroyEffect(AddSpecialEffect("war3mapImported\\Effect_setvang.mdl",GetUnitX(e7)*1.,GetUnitY(e7)*1.))
set vs=true
if vv>$A5 then
set vU=30
endif
call Q1(c4,e7,nA,cm)
call qj(e7,2.,$64)
endif
call GroupRemoveUnit(ia,e7)
endloop
call fL(c4,eK,-1000)
set cC=null
endfunction
function KR takes unit c4,real ij,real iJ returns nothing
local integer c2=dv(c4)
local player cC=co[c2-1]
local unit e7=null
call DestroyEffect(AddSpecialEffect("war3mapImported\\Effect_phuan1.mdl",ij*1.,iJ*1.))
call GroupEnumUnitsInRange(ia,ij,iJ,$104,ip)
loop
set e7=FirstOfGroup(ia)
exitwhen e7==null
if IsUnitEnemy(e7,cC) and not ds(e7) then
set vW[c2]=vW[c2]+.05
call qZ(e7,4.,$64)
endif
call GroupRemoveUnit(ia,e7)
endloop
if vW[c2]>0 then
call UnitAddAbility(c4,$41303333)
endif
set cC=null
endfunction
function KO takes nothing returns nothing
local integer c6=ay()
set Wp[c6]=Wp[c6]-1
if Wp[c6]==0 then
set WQ[Wa[c6]]=false
call a0()
call gn(c6)
endif
endfunction
function KM takes nothing returns nothing
local integer c6=ay()
local unit e7
if Wb[c6]==2 then
call DestroyEffect(AddSpecialEffect("war3mapImported\\Effect_phuonghoanglua4.mdl",Wx[c6]*1.,Wl[c6]*1.))
endif
call GroupEnumUnitsInRange(ia,Wx[c6],Wl[c6],$A0,ip)
loop
set e7=FirstOfGroup(ia)
exitwhen e7==null
if IsUnitEnemy(e7,WV[c6]) and not ds(e7) then
call Q1(Wm[c6],e7,nA,Wu[c6])
endif
call GroupRemoveUnit(ia,e7)
endloop
set Wb[c6]=Wb[c6]-1
if Wb[c6]<=0 then
set Wm[c6]=null
set WV[c6]=null
call a0()
call g1(c6)
endif
endfunction
function K0 takes unit c4,unit cP returns nothing
local integer c6=g7()
set Wm[c6]=c4
set Wo[c6]=dv(c4)
set WV[c6]=co[Wo[c6]-1]
set Wx[c6]=GetUnitX(cP)
set Wl[c6]=GetUnitY(cP)
set Wu[c6]=I2R(BlzGetUnitBaseDamage(c4,0))*1.8
set Wb[c6]=1
call DestroyEffect(AddSpecialEffectTarget("Abilities\\Weapons\\RedDragonBreath\\RedDragonMissile.mdl",Wm[c6],"hand right"))
call DestroyEffect(AddSpecialEffect("war3mapImported\\Effect_phuonghoanglua4.mdl",Wx[c6]*1.,Wl[c6]*1.))
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(WE,Wo[c6]),26)>=2 and at(30) then
set Wb[c6]=2
endif
call aR(c6,.3125,true,function KM)
endfunction
function K8 takes nothing returns nothing
local integer c6=ay()
local real ij=0
local real iJ=0
local unit e7=null
if W2[c6]>$6E then
set ij=am(WG[c6],$190)
set iJ=am(WF[c6],$190)
call DestroyEffect(AddSpecialEffect("war3mapImported\\Effect_phuonghoanglua4.mdl",ij*1.,iJ*1.))
call GroupEnumUnitsInRange(ia,ij,iJ,$A0,ip)
loop
set e7=FirstOfGroup(ia)
exitwhen e7==null
if IsUnitEnemy(e7,Ww[c6]) and not ds(e7) then
call Q1(WC[c6],e7,nA,WN[c6])
endif
call GroupRemoveUnit(ia,e7)
endloop
endif
set W2[c6]=W2[c6]-1
if W2[c6]<=0 then
set WA[W4[c6]]=false
set WC[c6]=null
set Ww[c6]=null
call a0()
call P8(c6)
endif
endfunction
function Rc takes unit c4,integer c2,real ij,real iJ returns nothing
local integer c6=P0()
call SetUnitAnimation(c4,"spell")
set WC[c6]=c4
set W4[c6]=c2
set Ww[c6]=co[W4[c6]-1]
set WG[c6]=ij
set WF[c6]=iJ
set WN[c6]=I2R(BlzGetUnitBaseDamage(c4,0))*1.8
set W2[c6]=$8C
set WA[W4[c6]]=true
call DestroyEffect(AddSpecialEffectTarget("Abilities\\Weapons\\RedDragonBreath\\RedDragonMissile.mdl",WC[c6],"hand right"))
call aR(c6,.25,true,function K8)
endfunction
function R7 takes unit c4,integer c2,unit cP returns nothing
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(WE,c2),27)>=2 and not WA[c2] then
call Rc(c4,c2,GetUnitX(cP),GetUnitY(cP))
endif
endfunction
function R1 takes nothing returns nothing
local integer c6=ay()
if not ds(WH[c6]) and Wj[c6]>24 then
call pd(WH[c6],1.)
endif
if Wj[c6]==26 then
call DestroyEffect(W3[c6])
endif
if Wj[c6]==24 then
call SetUnitVertexColor(WH[c6],$FF,$FF,$FF,$FF)
call fL(WH[c6],eq,-1.)
call fL(WH[c6],e6,-1.)
call PauseUnit(WH[c6],false)
call BlzSetSpecialEffectAlpha(WJ[c6],0)
call DestroyEffect(WJ[c6])
call DestroyEffect(W9[c6])
set WJ[c6]=null
set W9[c6]=null
call BlzSetSpecialEffectAlpha(W3[c6],0)
set W3[c6]=null
endif
set Wj[c6]=Wj[c6]-1
if Wj[c6]<=0 then
set Wh[WB[c6]]=false
set WH[c6]=null
call a0()
call Py(c6)
endif
endfunction
function Re takes unit c4,integer c2 returns nothing
local integer c6=PO()
local real Ws=GetUnitX(c4)
local real WU=GetUnitY(c4)
set WH[c6]=c4
set WB[c6]=c2
set Wj[c6]=30
set WJ[c6]=AddSpecialEffect("war3mapImported\\Effect_phuonghoangnietban.mdl",Ws,WU)
set W9[c6]=AddSpecialEffect("war3mapImported\\Effect_phuonghoangnietban2.mdl",Ws,WU)
call fL(WH[c6],eq,1.)
call fL(WH[c6],e6,1.)
call PauseUnit(WH[c6],true)
call pd(WH[c6],1.)
call PK(c4,Ws,WU)
call SetUnitVertexColor(WH[c6],$FF,$FF,$FF,0)
set W3[c6]=fj("war3mapImported\\Effect_phuonghoang.mdl",Ws,WU,$10E,aN(Ws,WU)+$C8)
set Wh[WB[c6]]=true
call aR(c6,1.,true,function R1)
endfunction
function Ri takes unit c4,real ij,real iJ returns nothing
local integer c2=dv(c4)
local real cm=I2R(BlzGetUnitBaseDamage(c4,0))*8.
local player cC=co[c2-1]
local unit e7=null
call DestroyEffect(AddSpecialEffect("war3mapImported\\Effect_nietbancast.mdl",ij*1.,iJ*1.))
call DestroyEffect(AddSpecialEffect("war3mapImported\\Effect_nietbanhoa.mdl",ij*1.,iJ*1.))
call GroupEnumUnitsInRange(ia,ij,iJ,$258,ip)
loop
set e7=FirstOfGroup(ia)
exitwhen e7==null
if IsUnitEnemy(e7,cC) and not ds(e7) then
call Q1(c4,e7,nA,cm)
call qJ(e7,5.,$64)
endif
call GroupRemoveUnit(ia,e7)
endloop
set cC=null
endfunction
function Rn takes unit c4 returns nothing
if not q7(c4) then
if pk(c4,$C8) then
call qc(c4,0,30)
call Re(c4,dv(c4))
endif
endif
endfunction
function RW takes nothing returns nothing

loop
set e7=FirstOfGroup(ia)
exitwhen e7==null

call Q1(Lr[c6],e7,LR,cm)
endif
call GroupRemoveUnit(ia,e7)
endloop
set Lr[c6]=null
set LK[c6]=null
call a0()

endfunction

endfunction

endif

function RH takes nothing returns nothing
local integer c6=ay()
local unit e7=null
local integer LT=12
if L5[c6]>17 then
call GroupEnumUnitsInRange(ia,GetUnitX(LD[c6]),GetUnitY(LD[c6]),$320,ip)
loop
set e7=FirstOfGroup(ia)
exitwhen e7==null
if IsUnitOwnedByPlayer(e7,eu) and not ds(e7) and e7!=LD[c6] then
call DestroyEffect(AddSpecialEffectTarget("Abilities\\Spells\\Human\\HolyBolt\\HolyBoltSpecialArt.mdl",e7,"origin"))
call pd(e7,.15)
set LT=LT-1
if LT<=0 then
call GroupClear(ia)
endif
endif
call GroupRemoveUnit(ia,e7)
endloop
endif
set L5[c6]=L5[c6]-1
if L5[c6]<=0 or ds(LD[c6]) then
set Lh=false
set LD[c6]=null
call a0()
call Pd(c6)
endif
endfunction
function RB takes unit c4 returns nothing

endfunction

endfunction
function RE takes unit c4 returns nothing
local real ij=GetUnitX(c4)
local real iJ=GetUnitY(c4)
local unit e7=null
local real cm=I2R(BlzGetUnitBaseDamage(c4,0))*3.
call SetUnitAnimation(c4,"spell")
call DestroyEffect(AddSpecialEffect("war3mapImported\\Effect_songxungkich.mdl",ij*1.,iJ*1.))
call GroupEnumUnitsInRange(ia,ij,iJ,$320,ip)
loop
set e7=FirstOfGroup(ia)
exitwhen e7==null
if IsUnitEnemy(e7,eu) and not ds(e7) then
call qJ(e7,5.,$64)
call Q1(c4,e7,nA,cm)
call DestroyEffect(AddSpecialEffectTarget("Abilities\\Spells\\Undead\\DarkRitual\\DarkRitualTarget.mdl",e7,"origin"))
endif
call GroupRemoveUnit(ia,e7)
endloop
set e7=null
endfunction

function RD takes unit c4 returns nothing
local integer c6=Pt()
local real ij=GetUnitX(c4)
local real iJ=GetUnitY(c4)
set nu[c6]=CreateUnit(eu,$6830334E,GetRandomReal(ij-$C8,ij+$C8),GetRandomReal(iJ-$C8,iJ+$C8),$10E)
call XZ(nu[c6])
set nb[c6]=R2I(25/iP)
call DestroyEffect(AddSpecialEffect("Abilities\\Spells\\Undead\\RaiseSkeletonWarrior\\RaiseSkeleton.mdl",GetUnitX(nu[c6])*1.,GetUnitY(nu[c6])*1.))
call aR(c6,iP,true,function RA)
endfunction
function R5 takes nothing returns boolean
local integer c6=ay()
if not ds(i4[c6]) then
call SetTextTagPos(iw[c6],GetUnitX(i4[c6])-iY[c6],GetUnitY(i4[c6]),iG[c6])
if iF[c6] then
set ig=R2I(GetUnitState(i4[c6],UNIT_STATE_LIFE)/GetUnitState(i4[c6],UNIT_STATE_MAX_LIFE)*$64+.1)
if ig<=25 then
call SetTextTagText(iw[c6],i2[c6]+"|cfff85555 ("+I2S(ig)+"%)|r",.0207)
elseif ig<=50 then
call SetTextTagText(iw[c6],i2[c6]+"|cffffff00 ("+I2S(ig)+"%)|r",.0207)
elseif ig<=75 then
call SetTextTagText(iw[c6],i2[c6]+"|cffafff54 ("+I2S(ig)+"%)|r",.0207)
else
call SetTextTagText(iw[c6],i2[c6]+"|cff7fff00 ("+I2S(ig)+"%)|r",.0207)
endif
endif
else
call DestroyTextTag(iw[c6])
set i2[c6]=null
set iw[c6]=null
set i4[c6]=null
call a0()
call PP(c6)
endif
return false
endfunction
function RT takes unit c4 returns nothing
local integer c6=PY()
local integer cV=GetUnitTypeId(c4)
set i4[c6]=c4
set iw[c6]=CreateTextTag()
set iG[c6]=$1F4
set iF[c6]=false
set iN[c6]=32
if IsUnitType(c4,UNIT_TYPE_TAUREN) then
set iF[c6]=true
set i2[c6]="[ BOSS ] "+GetUnitName(c4)
if cV==$6830344B or cV==$68303348 then
set iG[c6]=$2BC
endif
else
set i2[c6]="[ Triệu ] "+GetUnitName(c4)
if cV==$68303342 then
set iG[c6]=$226
else
set iG[c6]=$15E
endif
endif
set iY[c6]=80+StringLength(i2[c6])*2
call SetTextTagText(iw[c6],i2[c6],.0207)
call SetTextTagPos(iw[c6],GetUnitX(i4[c6])-iY[c6],GetUnitY(i4[c6]),iG[c6])
call SetTextTagPermanent(iw[c6],true)
call SetTextTagVisibility(iw[c6],true)
call SetTextTagLifespan(iw[c6],0)
call aR(c6,iP,true,function R5)
endfunction
function RC takes nothing returns nothing
local integer c6=ay()
local unit e7=null
call GroupEnumUnitsInRange(ia,GetUnitX(nt[c6]),GetUnitY(nt[c6]),$3E8,ip)
loop
set e7=FirstOfGroup(ia)
exitwhen e7==null
if IsUnitEnemy(e7,eu) and not ds(e7) then
if GetRandomReal(0,$64)<=50 then
call pd(e7,-.02)
else
call pd(e7,-.01)
endif
call DestroyEffect(AddSpecialEffect("Environment\\LargeBuildingFire\\LargeBuildingFire2.mdl",GetUnitX(e7)*1.,GetUnitY(e7)*1.))
endif
call GroupRemoveUnit(ia,e7)
endloop
if ds(nt[c6]) then
set nt[c6]=null
call a0()
call PN(c6)
endif
endfunction
function R4 takes unit c4 returns nothing
local integer c6=PF()
set nt[c6]=c4
call aR(c6,1.,true,function RC)
endfunction

function Rg takes nothing returns nothing
local integer c2=1
loop
exitwhen c2>4
if i1[c2]!=null and not ds(i1[c2]) then
call DestroyEffect(AddSpecialEffect("Abilities\\Spells\\Human\\Resurrect\\ResurrectTarget.mdl",GetRectCenterX(ie[c2])*1.,GetRectCenterY(ie[c2])*1.))
call SetUnitPosition(i1[c2],GetRectCenterX(ie[c2]),GetRectCenterY(ie[c2]))
if GetLocalPlayer()==co[c2-1] then
call PanCameraToTimed(GetRectCenterX(ie[c2]),GetRectCenterY(ie[c2]),0)
endif
set i1[c2]=null
endif
set c2=c2+1
endloop
endfunction
function Rt takes integer ii returns nothing
local integer c2=1
local integer in=0
if ii==1 then
set in=$1388
elseif ii==2 then
set in=$1B58
elseif ii==3 then
set in=$2328
elseif ii==4 then
set in=$2EE0
elseif ii==5 then
set in=$3A98
elseif ii==6 then
set in=$5208
elseif ii==7 then
set in=$80E8
endif
loop
exitwhen c2>4
if cY[c2]>0 then
call Qj(c2,12)
call Q9(c2,in)
call dc(co[c2-1],ic+"Nhận được "+I2S(in)+" Linh Thạch.",5)
call QD(c2,QE(),1,true)
if ii==1 then
call QD(c2,QE(),1,true)
else
call QD(c2,QA(),1,true)
endif
if ii==6 or ii==7 then
set cd[c2]=cd[c2]+1
call Xe(c2)
endif
if ii==5 and iv>=2 then
set cd[c2]=cd[c2]+1
call Xe(c2)
endif
if ii==4 and iv>=3 then
set cd[c2]=cd[c2]+1
call Xe(c2)
endif
if ii==3 and iv>=4 then
set cd[c2]=cd[c2]+1
call Xe(c2)
endif
endif
set c2=c2+1
endloop
endfunction
function Rm takes unit c4,integer e8 returns nothing
if GetUnitAbilityLevel(c4,$45654834)>0 then
set el[8]=el[8]-1
if el[8]==0 then
call d7(ic+"Vượt qua ải "+I2S(cF)+" Ngũ Hành Linh Giới, nhận được phần thưởng.",15)
call TriggerEvaluate(cw)
endif
else
if e8==i7[1] then
set el[1]=el[1]-1
if el[1]==0 then
set cN=2
call d7(ic+"Phụ bản Huyết Lâm đã vượt qua, tất cả người chơi nhận được phần thưởng.",15)
call Rg()
call Rt(1)
endif
elseif e8==i7[2] then
set cN=3
call d7(ic+"Phụ bản Thủy Vực đã vượt qua, tất cả người chơi nhận được phần thưởng.",15)
call Rg()
call Rt(2)
elseif e8==i7[3] then
set cN=4
call d7(ic+"Phụ bản Thạch Lâm đã vượt qua, tất cả người chơi nhận được phần thưởng.",15)
call Rg()
call Rt(3)
elseif e8==i7[4] then
set cN=5
call d7(ic+"Phụ bản Tiên Môn đã vượt qua, tất cả người chơi nhận được phần thưởng.",15)
call Rg()
call Rt(4)
elseif e8==i7[5] or e8==i7[6] then
set el[5]=el[5]-1
if el[5]==0 then
set cN=6
call d7(ic+"Phụ bản Lưỡng Giới đã vượt qua, tất cả người chơi nhận được phần thưởng.",15)
call Rg()
call Rt(5)
endif
elseif e8==i7[7] or e8==i7[8] or e8==i7[9] or e8==i7[10] then
set el[6]=el[6]-1
if el[6]==0 then
set cN=7
call d7(ic+"Phụ bản Bất Chu Sơn Quan đã vượt qua, tất cả người chơi nhận được phần thưởng.",15)
call Rg()
call Rt(6)
endif
elseif e8==i7[11] then
set cN=8
call d7(ic+"Phụ bản Tinh Không đã vượt qua, tất cả người chơi nhận được phần thưởng.",15)
call Rg()
call Rt(7)
endif
endif
endfunction
function Rx takes nothing returns nothing
local unit c4=GetTriggerUnit()
local integer c2=dv(c4)
if c2<=4 and cN<9 then
if ds(i1[c2]) or i1[c2]==null then
if cN>0 and not (GetUnitAbilityLevel(c4,$5A657235)>0) and cY[c2]>0 then
if cF>0 then
call dc(co[c2-1],ic+"Ngũ Hành Linh Giới ải "+I2S(cF),5)
endif
set i1[c2]=c4
call SetUnitPosition(c4,GetRectCenterX(P7[cN]),GetRectCenterY(P7[cN]))
if GetLocalPlayer()==co[c2-1] then
call PanCameraToTimed(GetRectCenterX(P7[cN]),GetRectCenterY(P7[cN]),0)
endif
endif
else
call dc(co[c2-1],ir+"Mỗi người chơi chỉ có thể vào khu vực Phụ Bản tối đa 1 nhân vật!",5)
endif
endif
set c4=null
endfunction
function Rl takes nothing returns nothing
local unit e7
call GroupEnumUnitsInRect(ia,cs,ip)
loop
set e7=FirstOfGroup(ia)
exitwhen e7==null
if GetUnitAbilityLevel(e7,$45654834)>0 then
call IssuePointOrder(e7,"attack",ao(cH),aV(cH))
elseif GetUnitAbilityLevel(e7,$41303245)>0 then
call IssuePointOrder(e7,"attack",LoadReal(eX,GetHandleId(e7),it),LoadReal(eX,GetHandleId(e7),im))
endif
call GroupRemoveUnit(ia,e7)
endloop
endfunction
function Ru takes nothing returns nothing
local unit c4=GetTriggerUnit()
local integer c2=dv(c4)
if not (GetUnitAbilityLevel(c4,$5A657235)>0) and c2<=4 then
set i1[c2]=null
call SetUnitPosition(c4,GetRectCenterX(ie[c2]),GetRectCenterY(ie[c2]))
if GetLocalPlayer()==co[c2-1] then
call PanCameraToTimed(GetRectCenterX(ie[c2]),GetRectCenterY(ie[c2]),0)
endif
if cN>1 then
call Rl()
endif
endif
set c4=null
endfunction

function R6 takes nothing returns boolean
local integer cp=cG
local integer c2=1
local integer e7=0
local integer ec=0
if cF==30 then
set cN=9
endif
loop
exitwhen c2>4
if cY[c2]>0 then
if cF==10 or cF==20 or cF==30 then
call Qj(c2,15)
set cd[c2]=cd[c2]+1
call Xe(c2)
else
if cF>20 then
call Qj(c2,3)
else
call Qj(c2,2)
endif
endif
if cp==1 then
set ec=GetRandomInt(1,2)
if ec==1 then
set e7=1
else
set e7=4
endif
elseif cp==2 then
set ec=GetRandomInt(1,2)
if ec==1 then
set e7=2
else
set e7=6
endif
elseif cp==3 then
set e7=7
elseif cp==4 then
set e7=3
else
set ec=GetRandomInt(1,2)
if ec==1 then
set e7=5
else
set e7=8
endif
endif

function Rz takes nothing returns boolean

call dP(c2-1,w2)
call BlzFrameSetVisible(wY[c2],false)

function yy takes nothing returns boolean
local integer c2=en
local integer cp=eJ
local integer e7=Cy
local integer G3=LoadInteger(e5,cp,G9)
local integer GZ=LoadInteger(e5,cp,Gh)
local integer GA=LoadInteger(e5,cp,GE)
local boolean iz=false
if G3>0 then
call SaveInteger(cq,IA(WE,c2),G3,LoadInteger(cq,IA(WE,c2),G3)+e7)
set iz=true
endif
if GZ>0 then
call SaveInteger(cq,IA(WE,c2),GZ,LoadInteger(cq,IA(WE,c2),GZ)+e7)
set iz=true
endif
if GA>0 then
call SaveInteger(cq,IA(WE,c2),GA,LoadInteger(cq,IA(WE,c2),GA)+e7)
set iz=true
endif
if GD[c2-1] and iz then
if G5[c2]==1 then
if G3<=7 or GZ<=7 then
call x1(c2,G5[c2])
endif
elseif G5[c2]==2 then
if G3<=14 or GZ<=14 then
call x1(c2,G5[c2])
endif
elseif G5[c2]==3 then
if G3<=21 or GZ<=21 then
call x1(c2,G5[c2])
endif
elseif G5[c2]==4 then
if G3<=28 or GZ<=28 then
call x1(c2,G5[c2])
endif
endif
endif
return true
endfunction
function yM takes nothing returns boolean
local integer c2=en
local integer LT=eJ
local integer c6=xi()
local integer cp=1
local integer iz=0
local integer wO=0
local boolean cP=false
set GG[c6]=-7+7*LT
set GF[c6]=c2
set GN[c6]=1
set iz=-5+5*GN[c6]
loop
exitwhen cp>5
set wO=LoadInteger(cq,IA(G2,GG[c6]+GN[c6]),cp)
if wO>0 then
call dg(GF[c6]-1,GY[iz+cp])
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(CO,c2),wO)>0 then
call aH(GF[c6]-1,GY[iz+cp],"war3mapImported\\HeroIcon_"+I2S(wO)+".blp")
else
call aH(GF[c6]-1,GY[iz+cp],"war3mapImported\\DisHeroIcon_"+I2S(wO)+".blp")
endif
call SaveStr(cq,IA(CO,GF[c6]),iz+cp,GetObjectName(DE[LoadInteger(cq,IA(G2,GG[c6]+GN[c6]),cp)]))
set cP=true
else
call dP(GF[c6]-1,GY[iz+cp])
endif
set cp=cp+1
endloop
if cP then
set GP=BlzGetAbilityTooltip($41303237,GG[c6]+GN[c6]-1)+"\n"
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(WE,GF[c6]),GG[c6]+GN[c6])>=2 then
set GP=GP+"|cffffff00"+BlzGetAbilityExtendedTooltip($41303237,GG[c6]+GN[c6]-1)+"|r"
else
set GP=GP+BlzGetAbilityExtendedTooltip($41303237,GG[c6]+GN[c6]-1)
endif
call dl(GF[c6]-1,Gg[GN[c6]],GP)
else
call dl(GF[c6]-1,Gg[GN[c6]],"")
endif
call aR(c6,iP,true,function QM)
return true
endfunction

call dP(c2-1,GV[1])
call dP(c2-1,GV[2])
call dl(c2-1,Gx,"")
set cp=1
loop
exitwhen cp>7
call dl(c2-1,Gg[cp],"")
set cp=cp+1
endloop
return true
endfunction

function Mc takes nothing returns boolean
local player cC=GetTriggerPlayer()
local framehandle cx=BlzGetTriggerFrame()
local integer cV=GetPlayerId(cC)
local integer Gb=LoadInteger(es,Gu,GetHandleId(cx))
local integer GI=LoadInteger(es,Gk,GetHandleId(cx))
call dg(cV,Gf)
if Gb==1 then
call aH(cV,Gd,LoadStr(cq,IA(w1,cV+1),GI))
call QI(cV+1,GI,1)
call dl(cV,GX,GP)
elseif Gb==2 then
call aH(cV,Gd,LoadStr(cq,IA(wi,cV+1),GI))
call QI(cV+1,GI,2)
call dl(cV,GX,GP)
else
call aH(cV,Gd,LoadStr(cq,IA(wL,cV+1),GI))
call QI(cV+1,GI,3)
call dl(cV,GX,GP)
endif
call dg(cV,Gd)
set cx=null
set cC=null
return true
endfunction

function M1 takes nothing returns boolean
local player cC=GetTriggerPlayer()
local framehandle cx=BlzGetTriggerFrame()
local integer cV=GetPlayerId(cC)
local integer Gb=LoadInteger(es,Gu,GetHandleId(cx))
local integer GI=LoadInteger(es,Gk,GetHandleId(cx))
local integer CK=0
if GetLocalPlayer()==cC then
call BlzFrameSetEnable(cx,false)
call BlzFrameSetEnable(cx,true)
endif
if Gb==1 then
set CK=LoadInteger(cq,IA(w1,cV+1),GI)
if CK<12 then
if iL[cV+1]-cb>=GQ[CK+1]*2 then
call Q9(cV+1,-2*GQ[CK+1])
call SaveInteger(cq,IA(w1,cV+1),GI,CK+1)
call QI(cV+1,GI,Gb)
call dl(cV,GX,GP)
call SaveStr(cq,IA(w1,cV+1),GI,"war3mapImported\\TB_"+I2S(LoadInteger(cq,IA(w7,cV+1),GI)+1)+"_1_"+I2S(LoadInteger(cq,IA(w1,cV+1),GI))+".blp")
call aH(cV,Gd,LoadStr(cq,IA(w1,cV+1),GI))
call aH(cV,we[GI],LoadStr(cq,IA(w1,cV+1),GI))
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(CQ,cV+1),GI)!=0 then
call Qk(Bs[(cV+1)*5+GI],1,1,1)
if CK+1==12 then
call fL(Bs[(cV+1)*5+GI],B9,.05)
endif
endif
endif
endif
elseif Gb==2 then
set CK=LoadInteger(cq,IA(wi,cV+1),GI)
if CK<12 then
if iL[cV+1]-cb>=GQ[CK+1] then
call Q9(cV+1,-GQ[CK+1])
call SaveInteger(cq,IA(wi,cV+1),GI,CK+1)
call QI(cV+1,GI,Gb)
call dl(cV,GX,GP)
call SaveStr(cq,IA(wi,cV+1),GI,"war3mapImported\\TB_"+I2S(LoadInteger(cq,IA(w7,cV+1),GI)+1)+"_2_"+I2S(LoadInteger(cq,IA(wi,cV+1),GI))+".blp")
call aH(cV,Gd,LoadStr(cq,IA(wi,cV+1),GI))
call aH(cV,wn[GI],LoadStr(cq,IA(wi,cV+1),GI))
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(CQ,cV+1),GI)!=0 then
call Qk(Bs[(cV+1)*5+GI],2,1,1)
if CK+1==12 then
call fL(Bs[(cV+1)*5+GI],vA,.05)
endif
endif
endif
endif
else
set CK=LoadInteger(cq,IA(wL,cV+1),GI)
if CK<12 then
if iL[cV+1]-cb>=GQ[CK+1] then
call Q9(cV+1,-GQ[CK+1])
call SaveInteger(cq,IA(wL,cV+1),GI,CK+1)
call QI(cV+1,GI,Gb)
call dl(cV,GX,GP)
call SaveStr(cq,IA(wL,cV+1),GI,"war3mapImported\\TB_"+I2S(LoadInteger(cq,IA(w7,cV+1),GI)+1)+"_3_"+I2S(LoadInteger(cq,IA(wL,cV+1),GI))+".blp")
call aH(cV,Gd,LoadStr(cq,IA(wL,cV+1),GI))
call aH(cV,wW[GI],LoadStr(cq,IA(wL,cV+1),GI))
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(CQ,cV+1),GI)!=0 then
call Qk(Bs[(cV+1)*5+GI],3,1,1)
if CK+1==12 then
call fL(Bs[(cV+1)*5+GI],vA,.05)
endif
endif
endif
endif
endif
set cC=null
set cx=null
return true
endfunction

function Mn takes nothing returns boolean
local integer c2=en
local integer cz=eJ
local integer GO=Cy
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(ee,c2),cz)>=1 then
if cz==27 or cz==38 then
if not Qm(c2,27) or not Qm(c2,38) then
set cz=0
endif
elseif cz==28 or cz==39 then
if not Qm(c2,28) or not Qm(c2,39) then
set cz=0
endif
elseif cz==19 or cz==62 then
if not Qm(c2,19) or not Qm(c2,62) then
set cz=0
endif
elseif cz==20 or cz==63 then
if not Qm(c2,20) or not Qm(c2,63) then
set cz=0
endif
elseif cz==21 or cz==64 then
if not Qm(c2,21) or not Qm(c2,64) then
set cz=0
endif
elseif cz>8 then
if not Qm(c2,cz) then
set cz=0
endif
endif
if cz>0 then
call SaveInteger(cq,IA(CQ,c2),GO,cz)
set Bs[c2*5+GO]=xh(c2,GO,cz,0,0,false)
call QD(c2,cz,-1,true)
set G0[c2]=Bs[c2*5+GO]
endif
endif
return true
endfunction
function ML takes nothing returns boolean
local integer c2=en
local integer CX=eJ
local integer cp=Cy
local real Cq=iH
local real C6=iB
local boolean Cz=wC
local real Ws=GetRectCenterX(ie[c2])*1.+$384*1.*Cos($B4*1.*bj_DEGTORAD)
local real WU=GetRectCenterY(ie[c2])*1.+$384*1.*Sin($B4*1.*bj_DEGTORAD)
local real ij=Ws*1.+$12C*CX*1.*Cos(0*1.*bj_DEGTORAD)
local real iJ=WU*1.+$12C*CX*1.*Sin(0*1.*bj_DEGTORAD)
local unit c4
if Cq!=0 and C6!=0 then
set ij=Cq
set iJ=C6
endif
set c4=CreateUnit(co[c2-1],DE[cp],ij,iJ,$10E)
call Qx(c4,c2)
call dl(c2-1,Cp[CX],"|cff7fff00100%|r")
call SaveInteger(ef,GetHandleId(c4),ed,c2)
call SaveInteger(eX,GetHandleId(c4),Cx,CX)
call V0(c4,1,LoadInteger(cq,IA(c0,c2),1))
call V0(c4,2,LoadInteger(cq,IA(c0,c2),2))
call V0(c4,3,LoadInteger(cq,IA(c0,c2),3))
call V0(c4,4,LoadInteger(cq,IA(c0,c2),4))
call V0(c4,5,LoadInteger(cq,IA(c0,c2),5))
call V0(c4,6,LoadInteger(cq,IA(c0,c2),6))
call V0(c4,7,LoadInteger(cq,IA(c0,c2),7))
call V0(c4,8,LoadInteger(cq,IA(c0,c2),8))
if not Cz then
call SaveInteger(cq,IA(CO,c2),cp,1)
call x7(c2,cp,1)
call aH(c2-1,Ca[CX],"war3mapImported\\HeroIcon_"+I2S(cp)+".blp")
call dl(c2-1,C0[CX],GetObjectName(DE[cp])+"\n"+C8[LoadInteger(i9,DE[cp],ey)]+BlzGetAbilityActivatedExtendedTooltip($41303237,cp-1))
call aH(c2-1,wc[CX],"war3mapImported\\FrameUI_herobackground"+I2S(LoadInteger(i9,DE[cp],Ds))+".blp")
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(w7,c2),CX)!=LoadInteger(i9,DE[cp],iA) then
call SaveInteger(cq,IA(w7,c2),CX,LoadInteger(i9,DE[cp],iA))
call SaveStr(cq,IA(w1,c2),CX,"war3mapImported\\TB_"+I2S(LoadInteger(cq,IA(w7,c2),CX)+1)+"_1_"+I2S(LoadInteger(cq,IA(w1,c2),CX))+".blp")
call aH(c2-1,we[CX],LoadStr(cq,IA(w1,c2),CX))
call SaveStr(cq,IA(wi,c2),CX,"war3mapImported\\TB_"+I2S(LoadInteger(cq,IA(w7,c2),CX)+1)+"_2_"+I2S(LoadInteger(cq,IA(wi,c2),CX))+".blp")
call aH(c2-1,wn[CX],LoadStr(cq,IA(wi,c2),CX))
call SaveStr(cq,IA(wL,c2),CX,"war3mapImported\\TB_"+I2S(LoadInteger(cq,IA(w7,c2),CX)+1)+"_3_"+I2S(LoadInteger(cq,IA(wL,c2),CX))+".blp")
call aH(c2-1,wW[CX],LoadStr(cq,IA(wL,c2),CX))
endif
endif
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(w1,c2),CX)>0 then
call xL(c4,1,LoadInteger(cq,IA(w1,c2),CX),1)
endif
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(wi,c2),CX)>0 then
call xL(c4,2,LoadInteger(cq,IA(wi,c2),CX),1)
endif
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(wL,c2),CX)>0 then
call xL(c4,3,LoadInteger(cq,IA(wL,c2),CX),1)
endif
call SetUnitState(c4,UNIT_STATE_LIFE,GetUnitState(c4,UNIT_STATE_MAX_LIFE))
call SetUnitState(c4,UNIT_STATE_MANA,GetUnitState(c4,UNIT_STATE_MAX_MANA))
set Bv=c4
set c4=null
set GM=Bv
return true
endfunction

function MZ takes nothing returns boolean
local integer c2=en
local integer cV=c2-1
local integer cp=1
if eB[c2] then
set Fu[cV]=0
set eB[c2]=false
call dP(cV,Fb[Fk[cV+1]])
set Fk[c2]=0
loop
exitwhen cp>70
call dP(c2-1,eH[cp])
call dP(c2-1,FI[cp])
call dP(c2-1,Fb[cp])
call dP(c2-1,Ff[cp])
call dP(c2-1,eA[cp])
call dP(c2-1,eD[cp])
call dl(c2-1,ej[cp],"")
set cp=cp+1
endloop
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(Fd,cV+1),1)!=0 then
call dP(cV,Ff[LoadInteger(cq,IA(Fd,cV+1),1)])
call SaveInteger(cq,IA(Fd,cV+1),1,0)
endif
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(Fd,cV+1),2)!=0 then
call dP(cV,Ff[LoadInteger(cq,IA(Fd,cV+1),2)])
call SaveInteger(cq,IA(Fd,cV+1),2,0)
endif
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(Fd,cV+1),3)!=0 then
call dP(cV,Ff[LoadInteger(cq,IA(Fd,cV+1),3)])
call SaveInteger(cq,IA(Fd,cV+1),3,0)
endif
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(Fd,cV+1),4)!=0 then
call dP(cV,Ff[LoadInteger(cq,IA(Fd,cV+1),4)])
call SaveInteger(cq,IA(Fd,cV+1),4,0)
endif
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(Fd,cV+1),5)!=0 then
call dP(cV,Ff[LoadInteger(cq,IA(Fd,cV+1),5)])
call SaveInteger(cq,IA(Fd,cV+1),5,0)
endif
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(Fd,cV+1),6)!=0 then
call dP(cV,Fa[LoadInteger(cq,IA(Fd,cV+1),6)])
call SaveInteger(cq,IA(Fd,cV+1),6,0)
endif
if LoadInteger(cq,IA(Fd,cV+1),7)!=0 then
call dP(cV,Fa[LoadInteger(cq,IA(Fd,cV+1),7)])
call SaveInteger(cq,IA(Fd,cV+1),7,0)
endif

call dP(cV,Fa[LoadInteger(cq,IA(Fd,cV+1),8)])
call SaveInteger(cq,IA(Fd,cV+1),8,0)
endif
endif
return true
endfunction

function MC takes nothing returns boolean
local integer ii=0
local framehandle cx
local integer c2=1
loop
exitwhen c2>4
if f8(co[c2-1]) then
set wA[c2]=GetPlayerName(co[c2-1])
call dl(c2-1,F0,wF+wA[c2]+"|r")
call BlzFrameSetVisible(wm[c2],true)
call BlzFrameSetVisible(wb[c2],true)
call BlzFrameSetText(wG[c2],wF+wA[c2]+"\n|r"+iK+"[ 40/40 ]|r")
set wk[c2]=GetRandomInt(1,24)
call aU(wb[c2],"war3mapImported\\Avatar"+I2S(wk[c2])+".blp")
call aH(c2-1,wI,"war3mapImported\\Avatar"+I2S(wk[c2])+".blp")
if c2!=1 then
call dg(c2-1,F8)
endif
set Nc[c2]=true
endif
set c2=c2+1
endloop
call BlzEnableUIAutoPosition(false)
loop
exitwhen ii==12
set N7[ii]=BlzGetOriginFrame(ORIGIN_FRAME_COMMAND_BUTTON,ii)
call BlzFrameClearAllPoints(N7[ii])
call BlzFrameSetLevel(N7[ii],7)
set ii=ii+1
endloop
call ac(N7[0],.03556,.03556,.37,-1.)
call ac(N7[1],.03556,.03556,.405,-1.)
call ac(N7[2],.03556,.03556,.44,-1.)
call ac(N7[3],.03556,.03556,.475,-1.)
call ac(N7[4],.03556,.03556,.51,-1.)
call ac(N7[5],.03556,.03556,.545,-1.)
call ac(N7[6],.03556,.03556,.58,-1.)
call ac(N7[7],.03556,.03556,.615,-1.)
call ac(N7[8],.03556,.03556,.65,-1.)
call ac(N7[9],.03556,.03556,.685,-1.)
call ac(N7[10],.03556,.03556,.72,-1.)
call ac(N7[11],.03556,.03556,.755,-1.)
set N1=d6(Ni)
call BlzFrameSetAbsPoint(N1,FRAMEPOINT_TOPLEFT,.008,.171)
call BlzFrameSetAbsPoint(N1,FRAMEPOINT_BOTTOMRIGHT,.15,.151)
call BlzFrameSetScale(N1,1.3)
call BlzFrameSetTextAlignment(N1,TEXT_JUSTIFY_CENTER,TEXT_JUSTIFY_MIDDLE)
set Nn=BlzCreateFrame("BoxedText",BlzGetOriginFrame(ORIGIN_FRAME_GAME_UI,0),0,0)
call BlzFrameSetVisible(Nn,false)
set cx=null
call VE()
return true
endfunction
